Method: PHR-VLA 引入一个轻量级辅助"未来头"（future head），在训练阶段将 VLA 的内部表示与从未来观测中提取的潜在动力学表征进行对齐，实现对规划视野的推理。该方法使用来自腕部相机或第三视角相机的局部、接触中心、块级（patch-level）潜在动力学监督信号进行训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上成功率从 84.1% 提升至 88.4%；在真实世界拆卸任务上从 63.3% 提升至 82.5%；在 Meta-World 上使用第三视角相机进行块级监督，成功率从 56.70% 提升至 57.8%。

Conclusion: 特权潜在动力学对齐提供了一种有效的训练信号，能够增强 VLA 策略的预期性推理（anticipatory reasoning）能力，尤其在接触密集的精细操作任务中效果显著。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and vision observations directly to actions. However, most VLAs primarily condition action prediction on current observations and lack an explicit mechanism for reasoning over future task dynamics, which is particularly important for fine-grained, contact-rich manipulation. We present PHR-VLA, a framework that enables planning-horizon reasoning in VLAs through privileged latent representations of future dynamics. PHR-VLA introduces a lightweight auxiliary future head that, during training, aligns the VLA's internal representations with latent dynamics extracted from future observations. Evaluation results demonstrate that local, contact-centric, patch-level latent dynamics supervision from the wrist camera improves success rate on LIBERO from 84.1% to 88.4% and on real-world disassembly tasks from 63.3% to 82.5%. Patch-level supervision from a third-person camera also improves performance on Meta-World from 56.70% to 57.8%. These results demonstrate that privileged latent dynamics alignment provides an effective training signal for improving anticipatory reasoning in VLA policies. Project website: \href{https://davoodsz.github.io/PHR-VLA.github.io/}{https://davoodsz.github.io/PHR-VLA.github.io/}

</details>


### [138] [One year in a forest: Analyzing the challenges of autonomous navigation in subarctic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27628)
*Matěj Boxan,Nicolas Lauzon,Veronica Vannini,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了在亚北极北方森林中对移动机器人进行长达一年部署的实地研究，评估了64公里数据上的九种里程计、定位和建图方法在跨季节条件下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在亚北极地区的应用前景广阔（如林业、采矿和环境监测），但该环境依赖GNSS或云计算并不可靠，且现有外部感知模态（相机、激光雷达、雷达）通常在结构化城市环境或无显著季节变化的环境中评估，缺乏对季节性变化条件下自主系统性能的深入理解。

Method: 在亚北极北方森林中进行为期一年的移动机器人现场部署，收集64公里的数据，评估九种里程计/定位/建图方法，并分析其跨季节性能；通过将位置漂移与特征和置信度权重分布相关联，分析视觉SLAM在季节变化下的退化原因；还进行了跨季节定位和雷达/激光雷达多季节Teach & Repeat评估。

Result: 实验表明环境显著变化会严重阻碍现有先进技术的性能，特别是自相似场景和高雪堆条件下系统脆弱性增加；复杂SLAM算法相比本体感觉基线在精度上提升有限但显著增加系统脆弱性；视觉SLAM对季节变化尤为敏感；激光雷达方法可成功完成跨季节定位，而雷达和视觉方法即使在同季节内也因匹配特征不足而容易失败。

Conclusion: 该年长部署研究揭示了现有SLAM方法在季节性变化环境中的局限性和脆弱性，并为该领域提供了宝贵的实地经验、挑战和教训，对推动亚北极环境下的自主机器人应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Subarctic regions have the potential to see increased deployment of autonomous robots in applications including forestry, mining, and environmental monitoring. In these conditions, an autonomous system's reliance on GNSS or cloud computing is precarious due to dense tree canopies and atmospheric attenuation, necessitating onboard sensing and data processing. However, established exteroceptive modalities, including cameras, lidars, and radars, are typically evaluated in structured urban settings or in environments that lack significant seasonal variations. To address this, we present a field report on a year-long deployment of a mobile robot in a subarctic boreal forest. We evaluate 64 km of data using nine odometry, localization, and mapping methods and assess their performance across seasonal changes. The performed experiments suggest that the environment changes significantly hinder the performance of state-of-the-art techniques, which show increased fragility when subject to conditions characterized by self-similar scenes or tall snowbanks. Additionally, complex Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) algorithms offer limited accuracy gains over a proprioceptive baseline while significantly increasing system fragility. Furthermore, by correlating the position drift with features and confidence weight distribution, we show that visual-based SLAM methods are particularly affected by the seasonal changes. Additionally, we investigate the task of cross-season localization in a prior map. While lidar-based methods successfully completed localization runs between seasons, radar and visual methods are prone to failure due to a few matching features between runs, even within the same season. Finally, we detail the challenges and lessons learned from this year-long trial, including a multi-season Teach and Repeat (T&R) evaluation using both radar and lidar-based pipelines.

</details>


### [139] [Distributed Model-Based Diffusion: Finite Horizon Contraction under Bounded Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27685)
*Seth Golembeski,Keith L. Gibson,Alexander Gross,Shreyas Kousik,Anirban Mazumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式模型扩散（Distributed Model-Based Diffusion），一种基于采样的模型预测控制方法，适用于高度非线性、非凸、非平滑的多智能体系统。论文证明了该方法在非凸多智能体问题上的收缩性和对通信延迟的鲁棒性，并在圆环交换、协作驾驶和空中对战三类任务中验证了其性能，相较集中式方法分别提升31%的makespan效率和25%的空中对战胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体轨迹协同优化面临非线性、非凸性和维度灾难三大挑战，现有方法通常依赖大量简化假设。此外，智能体间的通信延迟进一步增加了求解难度。作者旨在提出一种无需过度简化假设、同时能应对真实世界中通信延迟约束的多智能体轨迹优化算法。

Method: 论文提出Distributed Model-Based Diffusion算法，其核心是采样式的模型预测控制（Model-Predictive Control）框架，通过分布式扩散机制实现多智能体协同。作者从理论上证明了该方法在多智能体非凸问题中具有收缩性（contraction）以及对通信延迟的鲁棒性，为算法在实际约束下的应用提供理论支撑。实验测试了圆环交换任务、协作中保真度驾驶任务以及空中对战场景。

Result: 在加入通信延迟的情况下，与集中式Model-Based Diffusion相比，该方法在圆环交换任务中将makespan（完成时间）提高了31%，在空中对战场景中提升了25%的胜率。理论分析和实验结果共同验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: Distributed Model-Based Diffusion是一种适用于真实世界中具有通信延迟的多智能体非凸轨迹优化问题的高效分布式算法，在保持理论可证明的收缩性和鲁棒性的同时，在实际任务中取得了优于集中式基线的性能。

Abstract: Simultaneously optimizing the trajectories of multiple agents is a challenging problem plagued by nonlinearity, nonconvexity, and the curse of dimensionality. A collection of interacting aerial vehicles or self-driving cars in an intersection are examples of complex multi-agent systems that remain difficult to solve without many simplifying assumptions. The presence of communication latency between agents further increases the difficulty. In this paper, we analyze Distributed Model-Based Diffusion: a sampling-based Model-Predictive Control method suitable for highly nonlinear, nonconvex, nonsmooth, multi-agent systems. We prove contraction and robustness to latency for multi-agent, nonconvex problems, showing applicability to real-world constraints. We test the algorithm on a circleswap task, a cooperative medium-fidelity driving task, and in an aerial combat scenario. Despite the addition of latency, our algorithm improves circleswap makespan by 31% and increases aerial combat win rate by 25% compared to centralized Model-Based Diffusion.

</details>


### [140] [Coordinated Motion Planning for Multi-Arm Systems via Iterative LQ Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27726)
*Junyoung Kim,Hanwen Ren,Lei Zhang,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于迭代线性二次（LQ）博弈的框架，用于多机械臂运动规划。每个机械臂作为独立智能体优化自身目标，同时通过共享全局状态和碰撞约束与其他智能体交互。该方法通过线性化动力学和近似代价函数，利用Riccati反向递推求解局部LQ博弈获得反馈纳什策略，并引入可微的自碰撞与臂间碰撞惩罚项实现协调、防碰撞的轨迹生成。实验表明该方法在高维场景下能产生平滑、安全且高效的轨迹，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂在高自由度共享工作空间中的运动规划仍是一个基本且具挑战性的问题。集中式规划器可扩展性差，而分布式方法面临鲁棒性和安全性问题。博弈论公式化能有效建模智能体交互，有望克服上述局限，但目前其在关节式多臂系统中的应用仍然有限。

Method: 提出迭代线性二次（LQ）博弈框架：每个机械臂建模为独立智能体，基于共享全局状态和碰撞约束优化自身目标；通过线性化动力学和近似名义轨迹周围的代价，求解一系列局部LQ博弈；采用Riccati反向递推获取反馈纳什策略；在优化管线中引入可微的自碰撞与臂间碰撞惩罚项，实现协调且防碰撞的轨迹生成。

Result: 实验表明该框架能够在高维设置下生成平滑、安全且高效的轨迹，性能优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了微分博弈公式在多机器人操作中的有效性，为多机械臂协作运动规划提供了有力的博弈论途径。

Abstract: Multi-agent motion planning for high-degree-of-freedom robotics manipulators in shared workspaces remains a fundamental yet challenging problem. Centralized planners often suffer from poor scalability, while decentralized approaches face robustness and safety concerns. Game-theoretic formulations offer a promising approach for modeling agent interactions, potentially overcoming these limitations. However, their application to articulated multi-arm systems remains limited. This paper presents an iterative Linear Quadratic (LQ) game framework for multi-manipulator motion planning, where each manipulator is modeled as an independent agent optimizing its own objective while interacting with other agents based on shared global states and collision constraints. The method solves a series of local LQ games by linearizing the dynamics and approximating the cost around a nominal trajectory, with Riccati backward recursions yielding feedback Nash strategies. To address the challenges of articulated systems, we incorporate differentiable penalties for self-collision and inter-arm collision into the optimization pipeline, enabling coordinated, collision-aware trajectory generation. Experiments demonstrate that our framework produces smooth, safe, and efficient trajectories in high-dimensional settings, outperforming traditional methods. This highlights the effectiveness of differential game formulations for multi-robot manipulation.

</details>


### [141] [CAVE-NAV: VLM-Based Autonomous 3D Navigation in Underwater Cave Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27793)
*Zhenqi Wu,Yuanjie Lu,Yisheng Zhang,Miao Yu,Xuesu Xiao,Jaejeong Shin,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）与思维链（CoT）推理的自主水下洞穴导航框架，通过融合RGB图像、深度图和声纳垂直净空测量等多模态输入，推断可航行方向，实现无碰撞的3D安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下洞穴环境对传统自主导航构成严峻挑战：基于视觉特征的方法因视觉退化而失效，声纳建图可能产生过于保守的障碍表示，且通信限制排除了实时人工引导。需要一种不依赖密集视觉特征且能在受限环境中自主决策的导航方案。

Method: 提出一个利用VLM结合CoT推理的导航框架，从环境线索（光强度梯度、通道形态、几何复杂度）中推断可航行方向，通过多模态输入（RGB图像、深度图、声纳垂直净空测量）融合，支持受限洞穴通道中的安全3D导航。

Result: 在多种洞穴拓扑的高保真仿真中，所提出的框架成功完成了所有端到端穿越任务，无碰撞且保持与洞穴边界的安全净空距离。

Conclusion: 该VLM+CoT导航框架能有效克服水下洞穴环境中传统定位与建图方法的局限，为搜救、科学探索和紧急撤离等应用提供可靠的全自主水下洞穴导航能力。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in underwater cave environments is essential for search-and-rescue operations, scientific exploration, and emergency egress. Traditional navigation systems commonly depend on dense visual features for localization and mapping. In underwater caves, however, visual degradation can undermine feature-based localization, sonar-based mapping may yield overly conservative obstacle representations, and communication constraints preclude real-time human guidance. To address these limitations, we propose an autonomous underwater cave navigation framework that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to infer navigable directions from environmental cues, including light intensity gradients, passage morphology, and geometric complexity, captured through multimodal inputs comprising RGB imagery, depth maps, and sonar-based vertical-clearance measurements, thereby supporting safe 3D navigation through confined cave passages. High-fidelity simulations across multiple cave topologies demonstrate that the proposed framework completes all evaluated end-to-end traversals without collisions while maintaining safe clearance from cave boundaries.

</details>


### [142] [Plan Along the Way: Event-Triggered Foundation-Model Planning for TAMP Execution in Partially Observable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28075)
*Puru Ojha,Narendhiran Vijayakumar,Nav Singhal,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ROBUST TAMP，一个模块化的LLM/VLM引导的规划框架，用于在部分可观测环境中执行反应式任务与运动规划（TAMP）。该框架将基础模型规划器限制在当前可见的关系场景状态上，对生成的任务级动作进行严格的接口验证，并利用执行适配器处理场景特定的动作。将物体发现视为独立的重规划事件，在执行稳定后重建可见场景状态并重规划。在六个RLBench/CoppeliaSim的厨房和烧烤变体任务上评估了不同规模的文本LLM和VLM规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中进行操作规划具有挑战性，因为现有基础模型引导的TAMP系统通常假设完全指定的场景状态或仅在子目标/执行失败后才进行模型级重规划。当执行过程中出现新的任务相关或非目标物体时，这些系统无法有效应对，需要一种能够处理动态可见性的反应式规划框架。

Method: ROBUST TAMP框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将基础模型规划器限制在当前可见的关系场景状态上；(2) 对生成的任务级动作进行严格的"可执行接口"验证；将验证通过的动作路由到场景特定的执行适配器；(3) 将物体发现视为独立的重规划事件，在稳定执行时间窗口后重建可见场景状态，并使用已完成动作历史和结构化重规划事件上下文进行重规划。

Result: 在六个RLBench/CoppeliaSim的厨房和烧烤变体任务上进行了评估，涵盖隐藏物体、非目标物体发现、铰接容器交互和时间操控流程。比较了不同规模的文本LLM和VLM规划器，在相同的验证、执行、监控和重规划管线条件下，报告了任务成功率、部分目标完成度、发现/失败触发的重规划行为、隐式非目标物体处理以及规划器推理成本。

Conclusion: ROBUST TAMP通过约束基础模型规划器仅使用当前可见的场景信息，结合严格接口验证和场景特定执行适配器，以及将物体发现作为独立重规划事件的处理机制，实现了在部分可观测环境中有效的反应式TAMP，为处理执行过程中动态出现的未知物体提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Manipulation in partially observable environments requires planning under incomplete scene information. In such settings, an initially valid plan may execute successfully yet remain insufficient for task completion. Existing foundation-model-guided task and motion planning (TAMP) systems can generate useful long-horizon task decompositions, subgoals, or constraints, but they often assume having access to a fully specified scene state or invoke model-level replanning after a subgoal, refinement, or execution attempt fails. We present ROBUST TAMP, a modular LLM/VLM-guided planning framework for reactive TAMP where unseen task-relevant and non-target objects may become visible during execution. The framework restricts the foundation-model planner to the currently visible relational scene state, validates generated task-level actions against a strict executable interface, and routes the accepted actions to scene-specific execution adapters. Object discovery is treated as a distinct replanning event and, after a stable execution horizon, the system reconstructs the visible scene state and replans using completed-action history and structured replanning event context. Evaluations are performed on six RLBench/CoppeliaSim kitchen and grill variants involving hidden objects, non-target object discovery, articulated-container interaction, and temporal manipulation procedures. We compare text-only LLM and VLM planners of different sizes under the same validation, execution, monitoring, and replanning pipeline, reporting task success, partial goal completion, discovery- and failure-triggered replanning behavior, implicit non-target-object handling, and planner inference cost.

</details>


### [143] [Stay Seated: Learning Omnidirectional Humanoid Locomotion on a Passive Mobile Chair with Casters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28090)
*Kango Yanagida,Kazuki Miyazawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了人形机器人在被动移动椅子上的全方位坐姿移动（seated locomotion）。通过扩展标准站立速度跟踪环境，加入被动椅子模型、坐姿奖励、评审者仅观察椅子状态以及任务定制的接触设置，策略无需运动模仿奖励即可学习。在随机命令评估中，策略能在几乎整个20秒滚动中跟踪全方位命令，最佳坐姿策略在速度跟踪上甚至优于站立策略。消融实验表明足滑正则化（FS）降低能量消耗（CoT）但增加跟踪误差，需与对称正则化（SY）或命令课程（CC）结合避免陷入局部最优。方向解析显示CoT排序为向后<侧向<<向前。该策略实现零样本sim-to-real迁移到Unitree G1机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在站立时需持续产生关节扭矩，而人类在桌面工作时会将重量交给椅子支撑。作者希望研究坐姿移动操作（seated loco-manipulation）的第一步——在被动移动椅子上的全方位坐姿移动，以实现更节能、更接近人类坐姿工作场景的机器人行为。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 在标准站立速度跟踪环境中扩展被动椅子模型；(2) 设计坐姿状态奖励、评审者仅观察椅子状态（critic-only chair observations）和任务定制接触设置；(3) 策略使用演员-评审者（actor-critic）架构，演员仅使用本体感觉和速度命令，无需接触感应或椅子状态；(4) 不使用运动模仿奖励；(5) 进行2^3全因子实验比较对称正则化（SY）、足滑正则化（FS）和命令课程（CC）的影响；(6) 零样本迁移到Unitree G1实体机器人。

Result: 策略在随机命令评估中能在几乎全部20秒滚动中跟踪全方位命令；最佳坐姿策略在速度跟踪上优于站立策略。FS降低CoT但增加跟踪误差，部分FS-only策略陷入静止局部最优；FS与SY或CC结合可避免此问题。SY改善纵向运动中的双腿对称性。CoT排序为向后<侧向<<向前。策略实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，在Unitree G1上生成全方位坐姿移动。

Conclusion: 本研究首次展示了人形机器人在被动椅子上无需模仿奖励即可学习全方位坐姿移动，验证了正则化组合对避免局部最优的关键作用，并通过零样本迁移证明了方法的实际可行性，为未来坐姿移动操作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots with quasi-direct-drive actuators continuously generate joint torque while standing, whereas seated humans delegate weight support to chairs during desk work. As a first step toward seated loco-manipulation, we study omnidirectional seated locomotion on a passive mobile chair, requiring unfixed pelvis-seat contact and intermittent foot-floor propulsion of the robot-chair system. We extend a standard standing velocity-tracking environment with a passive-chair model, seated-state rewards, critic-only chair observations, and task-tailored contact settings. The policy is learned without motion-imitation rewards; its actor uses only proprioception and velocity commands, without contact sensing or chair states. In random-command evaluation, the policies tracked omnidirectional commands through nearly all 20-s rollouts, and the best seated policies could outperform the Standing policy in velocity tracking. Across four training seeds, a $2^3$ full-factorial comparison of symmetry regularization (SY), foot-slip regularization (FS), and command curriculum (CC) showed that FS reduced CoT but increased tracking error and that some FS-only policies converged to stationary local optima. Combining FS with either SY or CC avoided this failure without retuning FS, while SY improved bilateral leg symmetry during longitudinal motion. Direction-resolved analysis showed CoT ordered backward $<$ lateral $\ll$ forward, with planted-leg extension in backward and lateral motion and knee flexion following heel contact in forward motion. The learned policy achieved zero-shot sim-to-real transfer to a Unitree G1 and generated omnidirectional seated locomotion.

</details>


### [144] [DeicticVLA: Unifying Instruction Modes Based on Language and Deictic Gestures in a Single VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28108)
*Kango Yanagida,Tatsuya Aoki,Yuichiro Yoshikawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DeicticVLA框架，将语言指令（LI）、视觉语言指令（VLI）和视觉指令（VI）三种指令模式统一规范化为文本提示和指代掩码（deictic masks），使单个预训练VLA模型能够处理所有三种交互模式。在仿真和真实环境中验证了该方法的有效性，尤其在未见类别和未见表达上，VLI和VI显著优于LI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Vision-Language-Action模型（VLA）允许用户通过自然语言指定操作任务，但当需要在同类别或外观相似的物体中区分目标或放置位置时，需要非常详细的表达，而VLA可能无法可靠地使用这些表达。这促使需要一种能统一不同指令模式（语言、视觉语言、纯视觉）的框架，以降低用户表达负担并提升指代准确性。

Method: DeicticVLA将三种指令模式（LI、VLI、VI）统一规范化为"文本提示+指代掩码"的形式，通过文本提示补全和指代手势接地（deictic gesture grounding）实现。在仿真中，使用共享主干网络、演示数据和匹配训练步数，对比了两种RGB视觉提示方法、两种分离通道掩码提示方法以及三种训练策略（两阶段训练等）。

Result: 仿真结果：在两阶段训练下，四种提示方法在分布内任务上均取得高成功率，但在未见布局中使用指代掩码的能力存在差异。训练策略消融表明两阶段训练有助于提升掩码使用能力，且保留第二阶段的LI数据在不过度降低VLI和VI性能的情况下缓解了遗忘。真实世界三个任务中，单个策略支持所有模式；在未见表达、外观变化和新物体场景下VLI和VI优于LI。对未见类别，VLI和VI均达到100%成功率，而联合训练的LI仅16.7%。

Conclusion: DeicticVLA成功实现了三种指令模式的统一接口，验证了视觉指代信息（掩码）在提升VLA泛化能力方面的关键价值，并为VLA系统的多模态指令设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) allow users to specify manipulation tasks in natural language, but distinguishing a target or placement goal among objects of the same category or similar appearance requires detailed expressions that VLAs may not use reliably. We propose DeicticVLA, which canonicalizes Language Instruction (LI), Vision-Language Instruction (VLI), and Visual Instruction (VI) into a text prompt and deictic masks through text-prompt completion and deictic gesture grounding, enabling a single pretrained VLA to handle all three instruction modes. With a shared backbone, demonstrations, and matched training steps, we compare two RGB visual prompting methods, two separate-channel mask prompting methods, and three training strategies in simulation. Under two-stage training, the four prompting methods achieve high in-distribution success but differ in their ability to use deictic masks in unseen layouts. Across methods, training-strategy ablations show that two-stage training improves such use, while retaining second-stage LI data mitigates forgetting without reducing VLI and VI performance. In three real-world tasks, one policy supports all modes. VLI and VI outperform LI under unseen expressions, appearance changes, and novel objects. For unseen categories, both achieve 100% success, compared with 16.7% for jointly trained LI. These results demonstrate the unified three-mode interface and guide DeicticVLA design.

</details>


### [145] [Contact-Guided Exploration for Non-Prehensile Locomanipulation with Multi-Critic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28140)
*Simone Tolomei,Mayank Mittal,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini,Paolo Salaris,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种接触引导探索策略，在Multi-Critic强化学习框架下，通过专门的探索评论员以密集接触奖励引导端到端运动至有效接触点，并逐步衰减其影响以恢复任务最优策略，最终在四足移动操作平台上验证了非抓取操作的真实世界部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取操作（如推动、搬运）对移动操作平台而言是多功能的技能，但此类任务涉及复杂的混合动力学和接触稀疏性问题，导致基于模型和无模型的方法都难以有效解决。

Method: 提出接触引导探索策略，在Multi-Critic强化学习框架中训练一个专门的探索评论员，使用密集接触奖励引导末端执行器探索有效接触点；通过逐步衰减该奖励的影响来恢复任务最优策略；利用通用抓取算法获取候选交互点以提升跨物体几何的泛化能力。

Result: 在箱体推动、椅子搬运和洗碗机开门等多个任务上进行了评估，并在四足移动操作平台上通过大量实验验证了椅子搬运策略，展示了真实世界中可部署的非抓取操作能力。

Conclusion: 多评论员框架中的接触引导探索策略能够有效缓解非抓取操作中的接触稀疏性问题，并通过真实机器人实验证明了该方法的实用性与可部署性。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation offers versatile skills for moving and rearranging heavy or bulky objects, particularly when combined with a mobile manipulation platform. However, both model-based and model-free approaches struggle with the complex hybrid dynamics and the sparsity of the contact in these tasks. To address these challenges, we propose a contact-guided exploration strategy implemented within a Multi-Critic Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework. A dedicated exploration critic is trained with a dense contact-seeking reward that guides the end-effector toward meaningful contact points; its influence is progressively decayed to recover a task-optimal policy. We obtain candidate interaction points from a general-purpose grasping algorithm, enabling the exploration mechanism to generalise across various object geometries. We evaluate the approach on multiple tasks, including box pushing, chair transportation, and a dishwasher opening task. Finally, we validate the chair transportation policy through extensive experiments on a quadrupedal mobile manipulator, demonstrating deployable non-prehensile manipulation in the real world.

</details>


### [146] [From Small Talk to Rapport: Exploring Robot Self-Disclosure in Collaborative Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28154)
*Kaitlynn Taylor Pineda,Anvii Mishra,Brian Chien,Angela Guo,Toluwani Williams,Ziang Xiao,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了人机协作中机器人自我披露（self-disclosure）策略对团队动态的影响。作者开发了基于LLM驱动的工业机械臂，设计了低披露和高披露两种闲聊策略进行用户研究（N=50），意外发现低披露条件下参与者披露更多并报告更强的团队协作和协调感，且该效应在先前有机器人协作经验的用户中更为显著，表明增加机器人自我披露并不必然促进融洽关系和互惠披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在与他人协作时会自然地闲聊并分享个人信息以建立融洽关系和维持社会联系。随着机器人越来越多地与人类协作工作，有效运用这些社交行为来增强参与度和支持团队协作变得越来越重要。虽然已有研究表明机器人主动发起闲聊有益于人机协作，但如何设计此类闲聊（特别是自我披露）缺乏深入了解。

Method: 研究者开发了一款由LLM驱动的工业机械臂，能够参与闲聊，并采用低披露或高披露两种自我披露策略。随后进行了50名参与者的用户研究，考察闲聊中自我披露对人机团队动态的影响，并对比有/无机器人协作经验的用户群体差异。

Result: 研究发现低披露条件下参与者自我披露更多，并报告了更强的团队协作和协调感，与高披露条件相比存在显著差异。该效应在先前有机器人协作经验的用户中更为明显。

Conclusion: 增加机器人的自我披露并不一定能促进融洽关系、社会联系或互惠披露。在设计机器人闲聊策略时，应考虑其他因素（如用户先前的HRI经验）对协作体验的影响。

Abstract: People naturally chat while collaborating and share personal information (i.e., self-disclose) to build rapport and maintain social connections. As robots are increasingly developed to work with people, the effective use of these social behaviors to enhance engagement and support teamwork becomes ever more important. While prior work has shown that robot-initiated small talk can benefit human-robot collaboration, less is known about how best to design such small talk. In this work, we explore how self-disclosure may be designed to support small talk within a human-robot team---especially when the robot is an industrial manipulator that lacks anthropomorphic cues and performs physical work. We first developed an LLM-driven manipulator capable of partaking in small talk, adopting either a low-disclosure or high-disclosure strategy. We then conducted a user study (N = 50) to investigate how self-disclosure in small talk influences human-robot dynamics. Unexpectedly, participants disclosed more in the low-disclosure condition and reported stronger teaming and coordination than those in the high-disclosure condition. This effect was more pronounced among users with prior experience teaming with robots. These results suggest that increasing robot self-disclosure does not necessarily foster rapport, social connection, or reciprocal disclosure; other factors, such as prior HRI experience, should be considered.

</details>


### [147] [Picking Bins Empty: A Hierarchical Hybrid Approach with Online Self-Learning of Grasp Points for Reliable Industrial Bin-Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28175)
*Florian Töper,Samarth Kishor Yelvande,Jan Niklas Ewertz,Rudolph Triebel,Peter Ohlhausen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种四层分层混合抓取方法，结合基于模型和基于无模型的抓取算法优势，通过在线自学习机制实现自主抓取点发现与排序，在三个汽车零部件上实现100%的料箱清空率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业料箱抓取（bin-picking）中，基于模型的方法精度高但易因遮挡或感知失败而死锁，且新零件需要大量人工标注抓取点；基于无模型的方法灵活但缺乏生产所需的可靠性和可重复性。现有工作将两种技术孤立对待，需要一种结合两者优势的统一框架。

Method: 提出四层分层混合架构：基于模型的管线作为稳健骨架，基于无模型的"探索代理"负责解决死锁情况和发现新抓取点；配合在线自学习机制，利用夹爪行程反馈和Wilson分数区间自主排序抓取候选，减少人工调试工作量。

Result: 在三个汽车零部件上验证，抓取成功率显著优于纯无模型基线；将基于模型基线的料箱清空率从50.9%提升至100%，在所有实验中实现完全清空。

Conclusion: 该分层混合方法实现了完全自主、无需人工干预的工业料箱清空操作，是迈向真正工业自动化抓取的重要进展，有效弥合了基于模型与基于无模型方法之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Bin-picking is a cornerstone of modern manufacturing, yet achieving complete bin clearance without manual intervention remains a critical challenge. While model-based methods provide high precision, they frequently suffer from deadlocks when predefined grasps are occluded or perception fails. Labor-intensive fine-tuning of grasp points is commonly required to reach a satisfactory performance for new parts. Model-free algorithms offer a more flexible alternative with "out-of-the-box" versatility but lack the reliability and repeatability required for production. Unlike existing work, which treats the two techniques in isolation, we propose a fourtiered hierarchical hybrid approach to combine the best of both worlds. A model-based pipeline serves as a robust backbone, while a model-free "exploration agent" resolves deadlock situations and discovers new grasp points. This is supported by an online self-learning mechanism that uses gripper-stroke feedback and Wilson score intervals to autonomously rank grasp candidates, reducing manual commissioning effort. Validation on three automotive parts demonstrates that our method significantly outperforms a model-free baseline in grasp success rate while improving the bin clearance rate of the model-based baseline from 50.9% to 100% across all experiments. This transition to full bin clearance marks a significant step towards truly autonomous, intervention-free industrial operation.

</details>


### [148] [PAMoR: Parameterized Affective Motion Generation in Real Time for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28213)
*Yan Pan,Lingfan Bao,Tianhu Peng,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAMoR将情感转化为可在机器人运动学上计算的定量控制参数，通过价效-唤醒（V-A）坐标以闭环形式从姿态扩展和运动能量中推导，无需人工标注即可作为生成条件，在29自由度Unitree G1上实时生成携带特定情感的全身运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感化运动生成方法仅适用于人类虚拟化身，且情感风格要么来自参考片段要么来自情感词，两者都无法被定量参数化，限制了情感运动的可控性和可操作性。

Method: 提出PAMoR框架，将情感转化为V-A坐标并直接在机器人运动学上计算（从姿态扩展和运动能量闭环得到），作为扩散模型的生成条件。模型包含一个动作先验和两个情感先验，在共享潜空间训练，在每个去噪步骤中组合：动作先验固定执行的动作，情感先验调节动作风格。全身运动在29-DoF Unitree G1上自回归生成。

Result: 生成的运动会跟踪全范围命令V-A值，同时文本到动作的保真度与纯文本基线相当。感知研究中，被试在38%的试验中正确识别命令情感，高于两个基线，接近人类身体表演的44%水平。

Conclusion: PAMoR证明情感可以作为量化参数直接在机器人运动学上计算和生成，无需人工标注，实现动作与情感均可编辑的实时全身运动生成，其情感可识别性接近人类表演水平。

Abstract: People read a humanoid robot's motion in social settings not only for the action performed but for the affect conveyed. Motion carrying that affect has so far been generated for human avatars, where style is taken from a reference clip or an emotion word, neither of which can be quantitatively parameterized. We present PAMoR, which turns affect into a measured control parameter: a valence-arousal (V-A) coordinate computed natively on robot kinematics. It is obtained in closed form from postural expansion and movement energy, and these measurements serve directly as generation conditions, with no human annotation. An action prior and two affect priors, trained in a shared latent space, are composed at each denoising step: the action prior fixes what is performed, the affect priors modulate how. Whole-body motion rolls out autoregressively on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 in real time, with action and affect both editable. Generated motion tracks the commanded V-A over its full range while text-to-motion fidelity still matches text-only baselines. In a perceptual study, raters identify the commanded emotion on 0.38 of trials, above both baselines and approaching the 0.44 reported for acted human bodies.

</details>


### [149] [Probabilistic Multi-Robot Gas Source Localization with Uncalibrated Sensors: A Distributed Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28214)
*Wanting Jin,Marc Zoel Arias Mitjà,Alcherio Martinoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分布式概率框架，用于多机器人系统中的源定位任务。该框架利用基于秩的特征（对传感器缩放和非线性具有不变性）实现无标定估计，通过专家乘积融合局部信念获得全局一致估计，并引入信息区域分配与路径规划策略提升团队协调效率，在高保真气体传感器仿真中显著优于标准测量聚合基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统中，当机器人配备未标定且异构的传感器时，其非线性和不一致的响应会导致可靠的信息融合变得困难，这使得环境状态估计（尤其是源定位）极具挑战性。

Method: 1) 每台机器人独立利用基于秩的特征估计局部信念，该特征捕捉观测的相对演化，对传感器缩放和非线性保持不变；2) 通过专家乘积（product of experts）公式融合局部信念，获得团队一致的全局估计；3) 引入信息区域分配与路径规划策略，减少冗余探索，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在高保真气体传感器模型的仿真中，所提方法在强传感器异构性下显著优于基于标准测量聚合的基准方法，实现了可靠的源定位精度。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了无标定传感表示可有效扩展到分布式机器人系统，为其在其他涉及异构传感器的估计任务中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Estimating environmental states with multi-robot systems becomes particularly challenging when robots are equipped with uncalibrated and therefore heterogeneous sensors, whose nonlinear and inconsistent responses prevent reliable information fusion. In this paper, we propose a distributed probabilistic framework for source localization tasks that enables calibration-free estimation in the presence of sensor heterogeneity. The key idea is that each robot independently estimates a local belief using a rank-based feature that captures the relative evolution of observations and is invariant to sensor scaling and nonlinearities. These local beliefs are then fused through a product of experts formulation to obtain a consistent global estimate across the team. To further improve the efficiency of team coordination, we introduce an informative region allocation and path planning strategy that reduces redundant exploration while balancing exploration and exploitation. We validate the proposed framework using high-fidelity simulations with realistic gas sensor models. Results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a benchmark method based on standard measurement aggregation, achieving reliable source localization accuracy despite strong sensor heterogeneity. More broadly, this work demonstrates how calibration-free sensing representations can be effectively extended to distributed robotic systems, paving the way for their application to other estimation tasks involving heterogeneous sensors.

</details>


### [150] [Training-free Suction Grasp Detection for Deformed Aseptic Cartons Using Vision-Language Models and Geometric Surface Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28246)
*Marin Maletic,Goran Vasiljevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的吸盘抓取系统，用于分拣变形的无菌饮料纸盒。系统将目标识别与抓取点选择解耦：利用开放词汇视觉语言模型检测纸盒，SAM2细化实例掩码，几何评分方法结合表面平坦度与法向对齐选择吸盘抓取点。对比了三种几何方法（KNN-PCA、Sobel叉积、RANSAC平面拟合），在真实机器人上跨三种变形等级和35个杂乱场景中，单目标抓取成功率达88.2%，端到端杂乱环境回收率为72.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可回收废物的机器人分拣具有挑战性，因为目标物体具有可变形和几何不一致的特性。传统的抓取方法难以应对变形纸盒这类形状不规则的物品，需要一种能够适应变形物体且无需依赖大量训练数据的抓取方案。

Method: 提出一个训练无关的吸盘抓取系统，将目标识别与抓取点选择解耦。流程为：开放词汇视觉语言模型根据文本提示检测纸盒；SAM2将每个检测结果细化为实例掩码；几何评分方法通过结合表面平坦度与法向对齐来选择吸盘抓取点。对比了三种几何方法：K近邻主成分分析（KNN-PCA）、Sobel叉积和RANSAC平面拟合。

Result: 在真实机器人上跨三种变形等级和35个杂乱场景进行评估，单目标抓取成功率达88.2%，端到端杂乱环境中的分拣回收率为72.6%。

Conclusion: 该系统在不依赖训练的情况下，通过解耦识别与抓取点选择，结合几何评分方法，能有效实现变形无菌饮料纸盒的吸盘抓取与分拣，展示了在可变形物体机器人分拣任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Robotic sorting of recyclable waste is challenging due to the deformable and geometrically inconsistent nature of target objects. We present a training-free suction grasping system for sorting deformed aseptic beverage cartons, decoupling target identification from grasp-point selection. An open-vocabulary vision-language model detects cartons from a text prompt, SAM2 refines each detection into an instance mask, and a geometric scoring method selects the suction point by combining surface flatness with normal alignment. Three geometric methods are compared: k-nearest-neighbour PCA, Sobel cross-product, and RANSAC plane fitting. Evaluated on a real robot across three deformation levels and 35 cluttered scenes, single-object grasp success reaches 88.2% and end-to-end retrieval in clutter is 72.6%.

</details>


### [151] [CoCoBench: A Cooperative Coordination Benchmark for Embodied Multi-Agent Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28266)
*Yang Chen,Ye-Xin Xie,Lirong Che,Danyang Peng,Yuzhe Yang,Peiwen Lin,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Lei Yuan,Jian Su,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoBench，一个面向多智能体具身协调的构造级（construct-level）基准，包含897个经过oracle验证的实例，围绕任务分配、顺序约束、互斥和交接协调四种协调构造进行系统评估，并在11个主流MLLM上验证了协调能力具有高度的构造特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体基准缺乏对多智能体协调的细粒度诊断。大多数基准要么聚焦单智能体任务完成，要么仅用整体任务成功率来概括多智能体行为，无法揭示重复工作、顺序约束违反、资源竞争和交接不同步等具体协调失败模式。

Method: 构建了CoCoBench基准，包含897个oracle验证的实例，围绕四种核心协调构造设计：任务分配（task allocation）、顺序排序（sequential ordering）、互斥（mutual exclusion）和交接协调（handoff coordination）。除任务成功率外，还提供构造级评分来衡量智能体是否有效协调。对11个主流MLLM在不同协调模式、观察输入和智能体数量下进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，协调能力高度依赖于具体构造：整体表现强的模型并不必然在各类协调任务上都均衡胜任，不同协调类型之间存在显著的性能差异。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计更具针对性的模型架构和提升多智能体协调能力提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Agent systems powered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced rapidly in recent years, yet existing embodied-agent benchmarks still lack fine-grained diagnostics for multi-agent coordination. Most benchmarks either focus on single-agent task completion or summarize multi-agent behavior with overall task success rates, which can obscure coordination failures such as duplicated work, violations of ordering constraints, resource contention, and desynchronized handoffs. In this paper, we introduce CoCoBench, a construct-level benchmark for evaluating multi-agent embodied coordination in executable household tasks. CoCoBench contains 897 oracle-validated instances organized around four recurring coordination constructs: task allocation, sequential ordering, mutual exclusion, and handoff coordination. In addition to task success rate, CoCoBench provides construct-level scores that measure whether agents coordinate effectively. We evaluate 11 leading MLLMs across different coordination modes, observation inputs, and numbers of agents. The results show that coordination ability is highly construct-specific: strong overall performance does not imply balanced competence across different coordination types. These findings point to new directions for designing targeted model architectures and improving multi-agent coordination ability.

</details>


### [152] [Spatial-Semantic Reasoning using Large Language Models for Efficient UAV Search Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28270)
*Marin Maletic,Marijana Peti,Tamara Petrovic,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无人机实时语义导航的框架，利用大语言模型解析自然语言指令并进行语义推理，结合实时目标检测、3D空间建图和多项式样条插值，在ObjectNav任务中显著缩短任务时间并保持高搜索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ObjectNav方法依赖离线推理或受限于模拟器约束的动作空间，缺乏实时性，无法在真实无人机环境下高效完成目标导航任务。

Method: 结合大语言模型（自然语言指令解析与语义推理）、实时目标检测、3D空间建图以及多项式样条插值轨迹规划，根据新观测连续更新语义相关性以优先探索高概率搜索区域。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景实验中，相比先验方法缩短了任务执行时间，同时保持较高的搜索准确性。

Conclusion: LLM引导的语义推理能有效提升无人机ObjectNav任务的时间效率，验证了该框架在真实世界部署中的可行性。

Abstract: We present a real-time semantic navigation framework for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) focused on improving time efficiency in the Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) task. Central to our approach is a Large Language Model (LLM) that interprets user-provided natural language instructions and performs semantic reasoning over detected objects and spatial context to prioritize high-probability search regions. The system combines real-time object detection, 3D spatial mapping, and polynomial spline interpolation for smooth and feasible UAV trajectory planning. Unlike prior methods that rely on offline reasoning or simulator-constrained action spaces, our framework can operate in real time, continuously updating semantic relevance based on new observations. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings demonstrate reductions in mission duration while maintaining high search accuracy, underscoring the effectiveness of LLM-guided reasoning for time- efficient UAV-based ObjectNav.

</details>


### [153] [STEGNav: Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Reasoning for Multimodal Lifelong Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28279)
*Yang Chen,Zhenyu Huang,Wenbo Fu,Danyang Peng,Shi-Yu Tian,Kun-Yang Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STEGNav，一种无需训练的框架，将传统场景图扩展为时空事件图，通过空间轴（查询条件实例定位与占据感知探索前沿）和时间轴（轨迹感知双窗口记忆）来提升多模态终身导航的性能，在GOAT-Bench、HM3Dv1和HM3Dv2上分别达到66.3% SR/39.7 SPL和64.0%/69.4% SR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于状态中心语义场景图的多模态终身导航方法存在三个局限：无法区分相似实例、难以联合表示语义目标和探索前沿、不能有效利用导航记忆和轨迹经验。

Method: STEGNav将常规场景图沿空间和时间两个互补轴扩展为时空事件图。空间轴执行查询条件下的实例定位，并以可达性、路径成本和探索效用为特征联合表示语义目标与占据感知的探索前沿；时间轴采用轨迹感知的双窗口记忆，保留最近的决策-轨迹事件和已验证的跨子任务导航结果；最终通过VLM智能体对时空事件图进行推理，选择目标实例或探索前沿作为下一个导航目标。

Result: STEGNav在GOAT-Bench上取得66.3%的成功率（SR）和39.7的SPL，在HM3Dv1和HM3Dv2上分别取得64.0%和69.4%的SR。消融实验和错误分析验证了两个轴的互补效应。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的时空表示能够提升导航可靠性和跨子任务经验复用，表明时空事件图是对传统场景图在终身导航任务上的有效改进方案。

Abstract: Multimodal lifelong navigation requires an agent to autonomously explore unseen environments while sequentially completing navigation tasks specified by object categories, language descriptions, or reference images. Existing methods primarily accomplish these tasks by constructing state-centric semantic scene graphs. By treating scene graphs as persistent repositories of semantic observations, these methods struggle to distinguish similar instances, jointly represent semantic targets and exploration frontiers, and effectively exploit navigation memory and trajectory experience. To address these limitations, we propose Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Navigation (STEGNav), a training-free framework that extends conventional scene graphs into spatio-temporal event graphs along complementary spatial and temporal axes. The spatial axis performs query-conditioned instance grounding and jointly represents semantic targets and occupancy-aware exploration frontiers characterized by reachability, path cost, and exploration utility. The temporal axis employs trajectory-aware dual-window memory to retain recent decision--trajectory events and verified cross-subtask navigation outcomes. A VLM-based navigation agent reasons over the resulting spatio-temporal event graph and selects either a target instance or an exploration frontier as its next navigation goal. STEGNav achieves 66.3% SR and 39.7 SPL on GOAT-Bench, as well as SR scores of 64.0% and 69.4% on HM3Dv1 and HM3Dv2, respectively. Ablation studies and error analyses validate the complementary effects of the two axes, demonstrating that event-driven spatio-temporal representations improve navigation reliability and cross-subtask experience reuse.

</details>


### [154] [MaCoPlanner: LLM-Assisted Manual-Compiled Task Planning with Proactive Safety Verification for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28300)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xua,Mohammad Deghat,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MaCoPlanner是一个基于设备手册知识编译的任务规划框架，将手册转换为类型化中间表示，通过检索任务/状态相关证据支持规划生成，并在执行前对候选计划进行符号化滚动检验，检测违规并定位修复或拒绝。在独立评估oracle下最终违规率为2.7%，Level-2任务成功率从62.8%提升至84.4%，Level-3从25.9%提升至43.2%，并在控制器面板模拟器上验证了集成执行可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人工业面板操作不仅需要精确的控制定位，还必须遵守操作程序、安全规则和设备状态约束，这些约束分散在异构手册中，传统方法难以系统性地将手册知识与任务规划整合，导致违规率高、任务成功率低。

Method: 将设备手册转换为类型化中间表示；从该表示中检索任务和状态相关证据用于支持任务规划生成；在动作执行前对候选计划进行符号化滚动执行，并与程序和状态转移约束进行校验；检测到的违规行为被定位并返回进行针对性修复，超出修复预算仍未解决的计划被拒绝；独立的执行接口将验证过的符号动作落地为物理控制操作并更新设备状态。

Result: 在独立评估oracle下，MaCoPlanner最终违规率为2.7%，26.3%的运行在修复分析中被拒绝（超出细化预算）。与Raw-Manual基线相比，Level-2任务成功率从62.8%提升至84.4%，Level-3任务从25.9%提升至43.2%。在无工业负载的控制器面板模拟器实验中，展示了在代表性交互条件下的集成执行可行性。

Conclusion: MaCoPlanner通过知识编译+符号校验+定位修复的闭环框架，显著降低了工业面板操作的违规率并提升了任务成功率；但作者明确声明该工作仅证明集成执行可行性，并未主张工业部署就绪。

Abstract: Robotic industrial panel operation requires not only accurate control localization but also compliance with operating procedures, safety rules, and device-state constraints distributed across heterogeneous manuals. This study presents MaCoPlanner, a task-planning framework built on knowledge compiled from equipment manuals that converts equipment manuals into a typed intermediate representation, retrieves task- and state-relevant evidence, and uses it to support plan generation. Before actuation, candidate plans are symbolically rolled out and checked against procedural and state-transition constraints; detected violations are localized and returned for targeted repair, while unresolved plans are rejected. A separate execution interface grounds verified symbolic actions to physical controls and updates the device state. Under an independent evaluation oracle, MaCoPlanner achieves a final violation rate of 2.7%, and 26.3% of the runs in the repair analysis are rejected after exhausting the refinement budget. Compared with Raw-Manual, task success increases from 62.8% to 84.4% on Level-2 tasks and from 25.9% to 43.2% on Level-3 tasks. Experiments on a controller-panel simulator without an attached industrial load further demonstrate integrated execution feasibility under representative interaction conditions, without claiming industrial deployment readiness.

</details>


### [155] [PanelShield: Verifiable Closed-Loop Safe Planning for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28305)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xu,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanelShield是一个用于工业面板操作的、可验证的闭环安全规划框架，结合LTL与Safety FSM双重形式化验证，实现违规检测与定向修复，在仿真和真实机器人实验中显著降低违规率至2.7%，且延迟仅4.1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业面板操作是知识密集且安全关键的，执行必须满足操作手册和安全法规的约束。现有基于基础模型的规划器虽具备强语义能力，但缺乏可计算、可定位、可复现的违规检测与修复机制。

Method: 框架从任务相关的手册证据生成参数化动作原语序列，采用LTL和Safety FSM双重形式化验证分别确保跨步骤时序正确性和局部转移合法性；违规时输出包含最早违规步骤及原因的结构化反例，支持定向修复与重新验证。

Result: 构建了覆盖三种代表性工业设备面板的多层次长时程规划基准，在仿真和真实机器人实验中评估。相比仅用基础模型的规划基线，PanelShield在复杂安全约束任务上性能提升，违规率降至2.7%，总延迟4.1秒，真实实验验证了端到端可行性。

Conclusion: PanelShield为机器人面板操作提供了一种可验证的方法，在灵活性、安全性和可审计性之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Industrial panel operation is knowledge-intensive and safety-critical. Beyond control recognition and action generation, execution must satisfy constraints in operation manuals and safety regulations. While foundation-model-based planners show strong semantic capability, they typically lack computable, localizable, and reproducible mechanisms for violation detection and repair. To address this, we propose PanelShield, a verifiable closed-loop safety planning framework for manual-guided industrial panel operation. The framework generates parameterized action primitive sequences from task-relevant manual evidence and applies dual formal verification with LTL and a Safety FSM to enforce cross-step temporal correctness and local transition legality. When violations occur, it outputs a structured counterexample with the earliest violating step and cause, enabling targeted repair and re-verification. We build a multi-level long-horizon planning benchmark covering three representative industrial device panels, and evaluate the framework in simulation and real-world robotic experiments. Results show that PanelShield improves complex safety-constrained task performance over foundation-model-only planning baselines while reducing the violation rate to 2.7%, with 4.1 s total latency. Real-world experiments demonstrate end-toend feasibility. Overall, PanelShield offers a verifiable approach to robotic panel operation that balances flexibility, safety, and auditability.

</details>


### [156] [Cooperative Risk-Aware Exploration in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems Using Algorithmic Altruism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28409)
*Brooks A. Butler,Jair Certório,João P. Hespanha,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于博弈论的多机器人协同风险感知探索框架，受生态学利他行为（Hamilton法则）启发，通过相关度权重塑造代理人效用，在有限时域内优化轨迹，使低价值机器人主动承担风险以惠及高价值队友，在仿真与硬件实验中验证了减少冗余探索、改善间距和风险再分配的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在危险环境探索中不仅需要决定信息收集位置，还需考虑异质团队成员间的风险分配问题。现有方法缺乏对团队成员间利他耦合与风险再分配的系统建模，需要一种博弈论框架来协同解决信息增益、冗余惩罚和风险暴露的权衡。

Method: 提出一个基于Hamilton法则相关度权重的博弈论框架，定义Social Nash Equilibrium来根据代理间相关度修改效用函数；每个机器人通过投影梯度法在滚动时域内优化有限时域轨迹，最大化信息增益并惩罚冗余探索与风险暴露；代理异质性通过特定价值参数编码利他耦合，使低价值机器人内化其轨迹对队友的影响并承担风险。仿真与硬件实验分别验证算法性能，硬件端采用单积分控制器和barrier certificates跟踪规划航点。

Result: 仿真结果表明，利他规划的探索框架减少了冗余探索、改善了机器人间分离距离，并根据代理价值重新分配风险，同时保持可比的地图覆盖率。硬件实验中，规划航点被轮式机器人通过单积分控制器和barrier certificates成功跟踪。

Conclusion: 基于生态利他行为（Hamilton法则）的博弈论框架能够有效实现多机器人协同风险感知探索，通过效用塑造使团队内部根据代理价值合理再分配风险，在不牺牲地图覆盖的前提下提升团队整体探索性能。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are well-positioned for exploration in hazardous environments, but effective deployment requires deciding not only where robots should gather information, but also how risk should be distributed across heterogeneous team members. This paper develops a game-theoretic framework for cooperative risk-aware exploration based on ecologically inspired altruistic behavior. Each robot selects a finite-horizon trajectory to maximize information gain while penalizing redundant exploration and expected hazard exposure. Heterogeneity is introduced through agent-specific value parameters for encoding altruistic coupling, which is modeled through relatedness weights inspired by Hamilton's rule. We introduce a game-theoretic structure for trajectory planning that defines a Social Nash Equilibrium, which modifies the utility of agent actions according to agent relatedness. This utility shaping causes agents to internalize the effect of their trajectory choices on teammates, encouraging lower-valued robots to accept risk when doing so benefits higher-valued agents and improves team performance. We define an exploration utility for agents that rewards area coverage and uncertainty reduction, while also penalizing redundancy and risk, enabling projected gradient-based waypoint optimization in a receding-horizon planner. Simulations show that altruistic planning reduces redundant exploration, improves inter-robot separation, and reallocates risk according to agent value while maintaining comparable map coverage. We further demonstrate the approach in hardware experiments, where planned waypoints are tracked by wheeled robots using single-integrator controllers and barrier certificates.

</details>


### [157] [Linear Temporal Logic Translation via Human-Inspired Self-Constrained Reasoning for Robot Task Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28435)
*Haofei Hou,Fanxu Meng,Shunyi Zhao,Kairui Yang,Mengchen Cai,Lecheng Ruan,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Self-Constrained Reasoning (SCR)框架，通过将结构知识内化到模型的决策过程中，在满足域约束的同时保持对新指令的泛化能力，用于将人类自然语言指令翻译为可验证的形式化任务规范（如LTL），服务于机器人安全执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务通常是时间延展性的，需要精确指定子目标、约束及其时序关系，而人类常以自然语言传达指令，这种语言具有歧义性、欠指定性和上下文依赖性。现有基于LLM的翻译方法要么通过开放式推理（可能违反域约束），要么通过事后约束强制（可能破坏新指令所需的推理），两者存在权衡。

Method: SCR框架结合结构约束表示（structural constraint representation）与层次化决策制定（hierarchical decision-making formulation），将结构知识内化到模型的决策过程中，在形式上受约束的空间内引导推理，而非作为外部过滤器施加约束。

Result: 实验表明SCR同时提升了域约束满足率和泛化能力，提供了有效且可解释的将人类意图翻译为可验证机器人执行规范的方法。

Conclusion: SCR通过在决策过程中内化结构知识（而非事后过滤），有效缓解了域约束满足与迁移泛化之间的权衡，为自然语言到形式化任务规范（如LTL）的翻译提供了可解释且可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks are temporally extended and demand precise specifications of subgoals, constraints, and their temporal ordering. Yet human operators typically communicate such tasks in natural language, which is inherently ambiguous, underspecified, and context dependent. Translating human instructions into formal task specifications, such as Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), is therefore essential for verifiable and safe robotic execution. Existing LLM-based translators attempt to bridge this gap through open-ended reasoning or post-hoc constraint enforcement, but the former may violate domain constraints, whereas the latter can disrupt the reasoning needed for novel instructions. This paper proposes Self-Constrained Reasoning (SCR), a framework that mitigates this trade-off by internalizing structural knowledge into the model's decision-making process rather than imposing it as an external filter. By combining a structural constraint representation with a hierarchical decision-making formulation, SCR guides reasoning within a formally grounded space while preserving adaptability to unseen instructions. Experiments show that SCR improves both domain-constraint satisfaction and generalization, providing an effective and interpretable approach for translating human intent into verifiable specifications for robotic execution.

</details>


### [158] [ChainSplat: A Physics-Inspired Screw-Theoretic Model for Learning Deformable Linear Object Dynamics from Multi-View RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28570)
*Seungyeon Kim,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChainSplat是一个物理启发的框架，仅从多视角RGB视频中联合学习可变形线性物体（如电缆、绳索、软管）的3D几何、外观、运动学和动力学。它将DLO表示为刚性连杆通过转动副连接的开链结构，并与高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）集成，实现紧凑状态表示下的高保真渲染和实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO的精确操控依赖于对其底层动力学和3D几何的建模，但其高维构型空间和材料属性差异导致挑战。现有方法依赖多阶段流水线和辅助深度输入，在动态交互下易出错，且高维状态表示使基于模型的控制计算代价高昂。

Method: ChainSplat将DLO建模为由转动副（revolute joints）连接的刚性连杆开链结构，获得基于螺旋理论（screw theory）的解析模型，状态由关节构型紧凑参数化。将该公式与高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）集成，联合学习DLO的动力学、运动学感知的3D几何和外观，并能从任意状态进行高保真RGB渲染。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，ChainSplat在动态交互下的动力学预测、3D几何重建和RGB渲染方面达到了最先进的性能。此外，它支持实时状态和力估计，以及准确的基于模型的轨迹优化。

Conclusion: ChainSplat展示了物理感知表示结合可微渲染在DLO操控中的实用价值，为真实的机器人操控提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying the underlying dynamics and 3D geometry of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as cables, ropes, and hoses, is essential for accurate robotic manipulation, but remains challenging due to their high-dimensional configuration spaces and diverse behaviors arising from varying material properties. Existing methods often rely on multi-stage pipelines and auxiliary depth inputs, which are prone to errors under dynamic interactions, while their high-dimensional state representations make model-based control computationally expensive. In this paper, we introduce ChainSplat, a physics-inspired framework that jointly learns the 3D geometry, appearance, kinematics, and dynamics of DLOs solely from multi-view RGB videos. ChainSplat represents a DLO as an open-chain structure of rigid links connected by revolute joints, yielding an analytic, screw-theoretic model with a compact state representation parameterized by joint configurations. By integrating this formulation with Gaussian splatting, ChainSplat jointly recovers DLO dynamics, kinematics-aware 3D geometry, and appearance, while enabling high-fidelity RGB rendering from arbitrary states. Through real-world experiments, we demonstrate that ChainSplat achieves state-of-the-art performance in dynamics predictions, 3D geometry reconstruction, and RGB rendering across dynamic interactions. ChainSplat further enables real-time state and force estimation, as well as accurate model-based trajectory optimization, highlighting its practical utility for real-world robotic manipulation of DLOs. Accompanying source code and video are available at: https://chainsplat.github.io.

</details>


### [159] [Aero Hand Open: A Simulation-Ready Tendon-Driven Hand for Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28578)
*Nan Wang,Mohit Yadav,Jonathan Wulff,Aidan Rosenbaum,Kezhou Chen,Yuvan Sharma,Xu Dong,Yiwei Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍Aero Hand Open，一个仿真就绪的腱驱动拟人手，包含仿真模型、标注的驱动映射和强化学习包，使策略可在纯仿真中训练并直接部署到实体手上，无需微调或状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动手具有拟人性且成本低（电机可远离关节、单个电机可驱动多个关节），但其欠驱动传动系统难以在仿真器中建模，且同一缆绳驱动的关节无法独立控制，导致强化学习在仿真到真实的迁移上面临困难。

Method: 发布Aero Hand Open三件套：(1) 复现缆绳传动本身的仿真模型；(2) 识别出的驱动映射，连接仿真模型与电机命令的双向映射（包括拇指的三向耦合）；(3) 用于训练策略的强化学习包。策略在仿真中完全训练后可直接部署到实体手，无需微调和状态估计。

Result: 实现了策略完全在仿真中训练并成功迁移到实体手执行，无需微调，也无需依赖状态估计。同时发布了机械设计、仿真模型、识别映射、训练环境和部署栈。

Conclusion: 通过提供仿真就绪的腱驱动手及其配套工具链，解决了欠驱动传动系统仿真表征困难的问题，实现了从仿真到真实的无缝迁移，为腱驱动拟人手的强化学习研究提供了完整开源平台。

Abstract: Tendon-driven hands are anthropomorphic, and moving the actuators off the joints is what makes a hand of this capability affordable to build. Two effects produce that saving. Routing force through a cable removes the requirement that a motor fit inside the joint it drives, so smaller and cheaper motors suffice, and one motor can drive several joints through a single cable, so fewer motors are needed. They are also harder to learn on than a direct-drive hand. The underactuated transmission that produces the saving is itself difficult to represent in a simulator, and the joints one cable drives are not independently commandable. We present Aero Hand Open, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand that is released simulation-ready. Three things ship with it. A simulation model reproduces the cable transmission itself. An identified actuation map connects that model to the motor commands in both directions, including the three-way coupling of the thumb. A reinforcement learning package trains policies for the hand. Together they let a policy be trained entirely in simulation and run on the hand with no fine-tuning and no state estimation. We release the mechanical design, the simulation model, the identified mapping, the training environment and the deployment stack.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过留一策略覆盖度（leave-one-policy-out）与状态所有者机制为PGPSE中的并行策略分配边际覆盖信用，在不改变总奖励质量的前提下重新分配非负辅助内在奖励，以抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。实验在受控环境、7个公开离散状态基准及PGPSE原始协议的Room和Maze设置中均取得了正面的最终窗口增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE通过并行策略在环境副本中最大化状态熵来扩展状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵分数仅衡量集体探索，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余覆盖，导致策略间可能存在冗余访问，降低整体探索效率。

Method: MCC-PGPSE结合两种机制估计策略特定信用：(1) leave-one-policy-out覆盖度，衡量移除某策略后覆盖的损失；(2) state-owner specialization，即状态所有者专门化机制。该方法保留PGPSE的池化目标，根据信用分数重新分配非负辅助内在奖励（总质量不变），从而抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在所有测试设置中，MCC-PGPSE相比Entropy基线在归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度上均取得正的最终窗口增益。受控任务对比和固定套件公共聚合结果显著，五种子原始协议对比方向一致。消融与信用对齐控制表明大部分增益来自留一策略覆盖机制，而非非均匀加权、错配信用或神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 基于贡献条件的辅助奖励分配是提升离散状态空间中并行策略互补覆盖的一种可解释且有效的方法，支持将边际覆盖信用作为并行探索中策略间协作的信用分配方案。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [161] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后训练量化在大语言模型边缘部署中并非语义中性的操作，形式化了"验证-部署鸿沟"，并证明可借此构建量化触发的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型评估往往只在全精度源检查点上进行，量化部署不伴随等效重评估。由于量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度下的安全认证无法保证部署后行为的等效性，这一结构性漏洞可能被恶意利用。

Method: 提出量化行为等价类（QBECs）理论框架，证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性；基于三阶段对抗微调框架嵌入潜在恶意载荷，使其在FP16精度下通过验证但在INT8或4-bit量化后激活恶意行为。研究扩展至多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型，并进行了跨量化器可迁移性分析。

Result: 在战术机器翻译场景中，后门翻译模型在修复的FP16下零度量的好友-敌人语料破坏，但量化后高达85.02%的反转率；立场分类器在压缩后产生高达ΔBias=0.33的意识形态偏移。攻击持久性因量化方案与模型架构而异，并非仅由名义位宽决定。

Conclusion: 仅依赖源精度审计无法排除量化触发的恶意行为，部署配置必须纳入行为认证范围，方能为可信边缘AI提供保障。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [162] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMP是首个针对GDN和KDA架构语言模型中循环状态的后训练量化方法。它发现均匀量化在精度-存储权衡上表现不佳，并通过离线校准中结合量化误差能量和衰减持久性识别高风险通道，将其以更高精度存储而其余通道使用INT8。在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上，DAMP以9.9比特/状态值保持接近FP32基线的精度，减少69.1%的循环状态存储，加速循环状态更新内核最高2.01倍，降低全模型TPOT最高10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN和KDA架构通过固定大小的循环状态替代大部分层的KV缓存以减少推理内存，但循环状态通常以FP32存储，占用大量GPU显存，且更新受内存带宽限制，显著增加解码延迟。作者是首个研究此类循环状态后训练量化的工作。

Method: DAMP提出混合精度方案：首先发现均匀量化效果不佳（INT8/FP8已损害复杂推理精度，INT4/NVFP4接近零）；观察到量化误差能量集中在少数通道且状态通道的相对衰减强度跨提示和任务保持稳定。基于此，在离线校准阶段结合量化误差能量和衰减持久性两个指标识别高风险通道，对这些通道使用更高精度存储，其余通道量化至INT8。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上，覆盖数学推理、通用推理和代码生成共6个基准。在9.9比特/状态值精度下保持接近FP32基线的平均精度；循环状态存储减少69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高2.01倍，全模型TPOT降低最高10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP通过量化误差能量和衰减持久性驱动的混合精度后训练量化策略，在保持精度的同时显著降低GDN/KDA架构模型的推理存储开销和延迟，证明了循环状态存在高效率低比特量化空间。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [163] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块稀疏特征化器（BSF）的优缺点，发现它仍存在经典SAE失败模式（如特征分裂和组合），并提出若干架构改进，包括锦标赛Top-K选择规则以显著减少特征分裂，并将块范式扩展到crosscoder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为稀疏自编码器（SAE）的变体，以子空间块而非单一方向为原子单位，旨在捕捉视觉中常见的低维流形特征。但BSF仍受经典SAE失败模式困扰，需要深入分析其优缺点并提出改进方案。

Method: 研究BSF的优缺点，诊断其在特征分裂和组合等经典SAE失败模式上的表现；提出架构改进，包括锦标赛Top-K选择规则以降低特征分裂；将块范式扩展到crosscoder以支持跨编码器场景。

Result: 提出的锦标赛Top-K选择规则显著减少了特征分裂，块范式成功扩展至crosscoder架构。

Conclusion: BSF虽在低维流形特征上具有优势，但仍需架构层改进以缓解经典SAE失败模式；锦标赛Top-K与块式crosscoder扩展是有效方向。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [164] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现持续学习（CL）中的灾难性遗忘与模型合并（MM）中的权重解缠误差本质上是同一现象——任务干扰（task interference），即某一任务的参数更新会偏移模型在其他任务上的输出。通过层间Frobenius内积⟨ΔW_ℓ, J_ℓ(x)⟩_F对其进行形式化，并证明优化器可通过控制谱范数‖ΔW_ℓ‖₂来调控干扰上界。作者识别出Muon优化器天然具备该调控机制，将其替换AdamW后在多任务合并和持续学习基准上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CL和MM分别受困于灾难性遗忘与权重解缠误差，但现有文献一直将它们作为独立问题分别处理，而忽略了两者共享的底层几何机制。作者旨在揭示二者本质上是同一现象的两种表现，并从优化器的角度提供统一且原理性的解决方案。

Method: 首先形式化"任务干扰"为层间Frobenius内积⟨ΔW_ℓ, J_ℓ(x)⟩_F；理论推导出隔离谱范数‖ΔW_ℓ‖₂作为优化器可控制因素的上界，并通过逐模式（per-mode）分析验证该上界追踪经验干扰的主导部分；进而识别出Muon优化器天然通过构造控制该谱范数因子，从而同时收紧CL和MM的干扰上界。

Result: 在八个任务的模型合并基准上，使用Muon替代AdamW在三个CLIP骨干上平均准确率提升最高+5.02点；在持续学习方面，Muon在十个类增量学习协议、三个任务增量学习协议及11任务MTIL基准上均获得一致的正向提升。

Conclusion: 任务干扰是CL和MM共享的统一底层机制，优化器并非实现细节而是可调控干扰的关键角色。Muon作为原理驱动的优化器中心方法，可补充现有的CL和MM解决方案，提供一致的性能增益。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [165] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球面版本的Flower神经PDE求解器，通过预测切平面位移并沿大圆传输特征来实现空间混合，完全无需卷积。作者还发布了一个新的球面PDE基准套件，包含六个具有挑战性的数据集，Dandelion在所有数据集上表现最佳或次佳，且在高分辨率下优势更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程在球面上展开，但默认的科学机器学习架构是基于欧几里得空间的。将欧几里得架构应用于规则经纬网格会产生多种问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度产生畸变；2D FFT在傅里叶神经算子中错误地假设双重周期性；ViT中的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲了球面测地距离。现有的球面数据集要么太小且过于风格化，要么太大（如ERA5）不适合模型迭代。

Method: Dandelion是Flower（一种基于warp的神经PDE求解器）的球面版本。其核心思想是：每一层预测一个位于切平面上的位移向量，并沿大圆路径传输特征。通过完全在球谐域中实现层次化池化来构建U-Net-like结构。整个架构不包含任何卷积操作，仅通过球面坐标变换（即warp）实现空间混合。

Result: Dandelion在发布的六个新球面PDE数据集（包括改进的Galewsky射流、异常链式湍流、Cahn-Hilliard分解、球面Riemann激波、Held-Suarez干大气传输和全球海洋动力学）上均取得最佳或次佳成绩。在256×512分辨率下，Dandelion和Flower2D在单步预测和滚动预测中均占据前两名，且与基于warp的基线方法之间的差距随分辨率增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 基于warp的神经PDE求解方法在球面几何上是有效且可扩展的，在不需要卷积的情况下即可与现有球面架构（如DeepSphere、DISCO、SFNO、测地注意力等）竞争甚至胜出，特别在高分辨率下优势显著。Dandelion和其基准套件为球面科学机器学习提供了新的平台。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [166] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端自解释多标签图神经网络（SEMGNN），在统一框架中同时进行多标签节点分类和边掩码解释生成，利用标签间的相关性提升预测性能并生成更忠实、紧凑的标签条件化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，多数多标签图学习方法不具备训练时解释能力，而事后（post-hoc）图解释器没有显式建模多标签学习器中的标签依赖证据共享，尤其在标签对弱相关或负相关时，无法正确处理证据在不同标签间的共享与分离。

Method: SEMGNN在统一框架和训练目标中联合学习一个预测器和一个稀疏边掩码解释器。利用标签-标签相关性来改进多标签节点分类，并增强各标签的解释质量，使同一节点的不同标签可以由不同但连贯的结构性/相关性证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐和生命科学等领域的合成及真实多标签网络上的实验表明，SEMGNN在预测性能上达到有竞争力或更好的水平，同时提供更忠实且紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Conclusion: SEMGNN是多标签图学习领域中首个具备训练时自解释能力的模型，通过联合学习与标签相关性建模，有效解决了事后解释方法在标签依赖证据处理上的不足。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [167] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARSANN，一种基于局部曲率几何信息的自适应最近邻分类框架，通过估计数据流形的局部平均曲率来动态调整每个邻域的空间支持半径（高曲率区域收缩半径、平坦区域保持较广支持），替代传统k-NN中固定邻域基数的做法，在70多个真实数据集上显著优于标准k-NN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN在全局特征空间中施加相同的邻域基数（邻居数量），但真实数据的局部几何结构（如流形曲率）在不同区域差异显著，固定邻域大小无法适应这种几何异质性。现有方法要么只调整邻居数量要么修改局部度量，均未显式适应局部证据的空间范围，因此需要一种基于几何信息自适应空间邻域支持的方法。

Method: CARSANN包含三个关键步骤：(1) 使用TwoNN方法估计数据的本征维度，并通过主成分分析(PCA)构建本征表示；(2) 基于形状算子(shape-operator)的公式估计局部平均曲率；(3) 用曲率控制邻域空间尺度——高曲率区域施加更强的半径收缩，近似平坦区域保留更广泛的空间支持。该方法显式调整邻域的空间延展范围而非仅改变邻居数量。

Result: 在70多个OpenML真实数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN并与现有自适应最近邻方法相当。在控制相同基邻域大小的对比中，CARSANN在45个数据集的40个上取得更高平衡准确率，平均平衡准确率从0.6506提升至0.7528。对固定k=5的k-NN也保持优势，Friedman和Nemenyi统计检验证实改进显著。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可以作为有效的几何控制变量来适应邻域空间支持，提供了与基于基数(邻居数量)的自适应最近邻方法互补的新范式。CARSANN的成功表明，将邻域的适应维度从"数量"扩展到"空间范围"是可行且有价值的。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [168] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何自同构群的实验设计诊断方法，用于检测无监督表征对齐中的退化问题。研究发现，添加廉价近重复样本会掩盖设计缺陷，而通过最小非平凡重标记这一诊断指标，可以在数据收集前仅用一次函数调用就识别并规避对齐退化风险。在颜色实验中，该方法将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐（unsupervised representational alignment）旨在仅从几何结构恢复刺激一一对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群本质上限制了任何对齐方法能识别的范围，即存在固有的退化性（degeneracy）。现有的诊断手段（最廉价非平凡重标记）在密集采样场景下会失效，因为近重复样本的转置几乎零代价，导致对已发表设计的风险排序错误。作者希望将这种已知不变性转化为设计阶段的诊断工具和干预手段。

Method: 作者提出将最小代价非平凡重标记作为设计期诊断指标（costs one function call），该指标能在数据采集前评估对齐退化的风险。研究使用颜色来验证，因为颜色的候选几何具有闭式解（closed form）。对比了对称设计与不对称设计在不同重启预算下的对齐恢复效果，并计算了判别表征模型能力与恢复对应关系之间的相关性（r=-0.02），证明二者本质上不相关。最后，仅基于该诊断指标选择9种颜色，无需咨询任何学习到的表征，测试了对所有93个模型表征的对齐改进效果。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 密集采样产生的近重复样本使最小重标记代价趋近于零，导致它们对设计风险的排序与实际情况相反；(2) 对称设计在64倍重启预算下仍无法恢复对齐，而不对称设计在同一N值下每次都能恢复；(3) 判别表征模型与恢复对应关系两个目标几乎不相关（r=-0.02，基于3000个子集）；(4) 仅用该诊断指标选择9种颜色，使93个模型表征全部远离退化点，灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%，且模型、层、N和求解器均保持不变。

Conclusion: 几何对齐的退化风险是设计层面的结构性问题，而非算法问题。凡是规则设计（regular design）与候选几何的等距群（isometry group）重合的地方——包括均匀间隔的方向、音调或运动方向——都会出现同样的风险。作者提出的诊断方法在数据收集前仅需一次函数调用即可完成检查，是一种低成本、高收益的设计期干预手段。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [169] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自组织映射图（GSOM）的生成式持续学习框架，通过分布统计记忆实现无需存储原始数据的类增量学习，支持无监督的样例免费回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临灾难性遗忘问题，现有方法通常依赖存储原始数据或需要任务标签，这限制了模型的可扩展性和隐私保护能力。作者旨在开发一种无需存储原始数据、无需任务边界或类别标签的完全无监督持续学习方法。

Method: 基于增长自组织映射图（GSOM），每个GSOM单元维护自身的均值、方差和协方差估计。通过分布统计记忆实现无样例回放，利用这些统计量生成合成样本；在编码器-解码器配置中，通过祖先采样将合成样本解码回输入空间用于后续训练。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能上与监督式有存储技术的先进方法相当，同时持续优于无存储方法。在单类增量场景中表现尤为突出，并提供了TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet单类增量基准结果。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无监督、自适应、拓扑驱动的神经统计回放机制作为可扩展且灵活的持续学习方法的有效性，为无需原始数据存储的持续学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [170] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入SegBench-GC，一个受控的离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）分割不变性压力测试基准，探究日志轨迹中因管理性原因（而非终止）产生的分割边界对多步目标学习的影响。通过延续有效目标（CVT）作为对照，实验表明将人工分割误视为吸收状态会严重降低性能（PointMaze成功率从50.5%降至19.1%），而CVT能保持一致。该基准为诊断此类失败提供了标准化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL通常依赖轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标计算，但日志中的轨迹分割可能出于管理性原因而非真实的终止状态。现有方法默认将轨迹边界视为终止，这可能导致错误的价值传播。需要系统性地检验算法对不相关分割边界的鲁棒性，即分割不变性。

Method: 构建SegBench-GC受控基准：固定迁移样本、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置和评估，仅改变人工备份边界及边界是否保留延续值。CVT作为分割一致的对照——奖励累积在人工切口停止，但目标从其存储的后继状态进行自举。在PointMaze上设置35,000个人工切口、三种分割实现、三种优化种子进行匹配计数实验。同时使用Decoupled Q-Chunking代码库中独立的n步基线（n=25）在Puzzle-4x5上验证。通过目标级诊断验证分析目标差值的数值精度，并通过学习评论家诊断对比朴素处理和CVT处理下的评论家偏差。

Result: PointMaze实验中：未分割成功率50.5%，CVT为39.1%，将切口视为吸收态时仅19.1%；跨分割实现，朴素处理的平均成功率范围从4.8%到31.9%。Puzzle-4x5上独立n步基线显示相同失败模式：未分割47.2%，CVT 58.5%，朴素处理仅0.27%。目标级诊断验证了分析目标差值的数值精度，学习评论家诊断显示朴素处理下评论家出现大幅乐观偏移，而CVT与未分割评论家近似对齐。

Conclusion: CVT使用标准延续自举而非新Bellman规则，本文的贡献在于提供了受控基准、失败隔离机制以及跨学习器的证据，表明行政性分割可以显著改变多步离线GCRL的行为。算法在实际部署中需区分真实终止与管理性分割，以防性能严重退化。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor.
