Result: 各机制对泛化的贡献不均等：稳态（homeostasis）提供了最强且最一致的收益，结构稀疏化（structural sparsification）为第二大有效机制；其余机制（输入门控、增益调制、阈值调制、侧抑制、激活去相关）在当前实验中效果较小或不够一致。两个问题均支持"显式调控神经元利用率和有效连接"这一统一原则。

Conclusion: 生物启发的活动调控和自适应稀疏化有望加速可泛化内部计算的涌现，值得在更广泛的场景（尤其是大语言模型）中进行深入研究，可能减少优化时间并加速泛化表征的发展。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization that is often accompanied by substantial reorganization of internal representations. This paper studies whether biologically inspired mechanisms, many of which are not commonly incorporated into artificial neural networks, can actively promote this transition by regulating hidden-layer computation at the levels of neuronal activity, response, and effective connectivity. We augment a multilayer perceptron with input gating, structural plasticity, gain modulation, threshold modulation, homeostasis, lateral inhibition, and activation decorrelation, and evaluate these mechanisms through systematic ablations on two established grokking benchmarks: sparse parity and noisy XOR classification. The results show that the mechanisms contribute unequally to generalization. Homeostasis provides the strongest and most consistent benefit, while structural sparsification emerges as the second major mechanism. The remaining biologically inspired mechanisms have smaller or less consistent effects in the present experiments. For both problems, the results support the common principle that explicit regulation of neuron utilization and effective connectivity can improve the emergence of generalizable internal computation. These findings motivate broader investigation of biologically inspired activity regulation and adaptive sparsification, including in large language models, where they may accelerate the development of generalizable representations and reduce the optimization time required for robust generalization.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Flat Netlist: Hierarchical Graph Representation Learning for Scalable Analysis of Sequential Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28188)
*Jingyi Zhou,Zhengyuan Shi,Jiaying Zhu,Ziyang Zheng,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepSeq3是一个层次化电路表示学习框架，将电路抽象为细粒度组合子图与高层Super-Node Graph两级表示，通过双GNN架构和状态中心预训练方案学习寄存器级时序动态，在BMC求解上实现18%的时间缩减同时保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路表示学习（CRL）方法尽管能指导EDA任务，但面临两大障碍：工业网表规模庞大导致难以实际应用，且未能显式建模寄存器级别的时序动态。

Method: 提出两级层次化表示：以触发器（FFs）为界划分细粒度组合子图，并构建高层Super-Node Graph（SNG）建模寄存器传输结构。采用双GNN架构分别在两级学习表示，捕获局部布尔逻辑与全局状态转移。引入状态中心预训练方案，通过预测FF状态间的可达性，赋予模型对时序行为的深入理解。

Result: 在大规模基准测试上，DeepSeq3展现出优越的可扩展性和更丰富的表示能力，将有界模型检测（BMC）求解时间降低18%，同时保证正确性。

Conclusion: DeepSeq3通过层次化抽象和状态中心预训练有效解决了CRL在工业规模网表上的可扩展性问题，并显式建模了寄存器级时序动态，验证了其在加速形式化验证任务中的实际价值。

Abstract: Circuit Representation Learning (CRL) offers a powerful paradigm to guide and optimize core Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tasks, but its practical adoption is hindered by the immense scale of industrial netlists and a failure to explicitly model register-level temporal dynamics. To overcome these barriers, we introduce DeepSeq3, a novel hierarchical framework that abstracts circuits into a two-level representation: fine-grained combinational subgraphs partitioned by flip-flops (FFs), and a high-level Super-Node Graph (SNG) that models the register-transfer structure. A dual Graph Neural Network (GNN) architecture learns representations at both levels, capturing local Boolean logic and global state transitions. Crucially, we introduce a state-centric pre-training scheme that predicts the reachability between FF states, endowing the model with a deep understanding of temporal behavior. Demonstrated on large-scale benchmarks, DeepSeq3's approach yields superior scalability and richer representations, reducing bounded model checking (BMC) solving time by 18% while guaranteeing correctness.

</details>


### [205] [Performative Privacy: When Differential Privacy Maximizes Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28198)
*Uddalak Mukherjee,Edwige Cyffers,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将差分隐私与表现型学习（performative learning）框架结合，提出"表现型隐私"概念，研究数据泄露如何通过降低未来参与度来影响学习系统的长期效用，并证明在反馈回路足够强时，有限隐私预算的隐私保护机制在长期效用上可以超越非隐私估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护学习通常以"保护数据可维持信任和参与度从而提升长期效用"为动机，但这一主张从未被正式形式化。作者希望将隐私保护与表现型学习结合起来，严格分析隐私预算与未来参与度之间的权衡，为差分隐私在长期效用层面的优越性提供理论依据。

Method: 提出"表现型隐私"概念（数据泄露会减少未来参与度），在均值估计的简单模型中，通过差分隐私机制实现隐私保护，建立泄露-参与反馈回路，并通过理论动力系统分析和数值实验研究隐私预算、估计噪声与长期参与度之间的权衡。

Result: 理论分析和数值实验均表明，当泄露与参与之间的反馈回路足够强时，有限隐私预算的差分私有估计在长期效用上可以优于非私有估计，首次为差分隐私在长期效用上的最优性提供了证据。

Conclusion: 差分隐私不仅是一种数据保护机制，而且在长期效用视角下也可能是最优选择，这为隐私保护学习提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving learning is often motivated by the idea that protecting users' data can preserve trust and thus participation, improving utility in the long term. However, this claim has not been formalized so far. In parallel, performative learning provides a framework for studying learning systems whose deployment affects the data they later observe. In this work, we bring these two perspectives together and introduce \emph{performative privacy}, where data leakage reduces future participation. We study a simple model where agents repeatedly contribute data for mean estimation but may leave the system when their data is leaked. Privacy is implemented through differentially private mechanisms, creating a trade-off between estimation noise and future participation. We show, through a theoretical study of the dynamics and numerical experiments, that a finite privacy budget can outperform non-private estimation in the long term when the feedback loop between leakage and participation is sufficiently strong. This provides first evidence that differential privacy can be optimal not only as a protection mechanism, but also from the perspective of long-term utility.

</details>


### [206] [Generalized Context in Cross Attention for Transfer Learning of Disjoint Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28209)
*Kazi F. Akhter,Ibna Kowsar,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATTLE（跨域注意力迁移学习）方法，通过引入广义上下文学习，利用源域中Transformer的key/value/query投影权重（而非激活值）捕获规则级通用知识，从而在无需共享特征的前提下实现跨表格数据的知识迁移。实验在10对不相交的数据集上验证，CATTLE在平均排名（2.9）和平均AUROC提升（3.7%）上均优于9个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的迁移学习因不同领域间特征类型、结构及语义的异质性而面临挑战。现有方法假设源域与目标域共享特征，这在现实中不成立。本文旨在去除共享特征的假设，实现数据无关的跨领域表格知识迁移。

Method: CATTLE通过Transformer的key、value、query投影权重来捕获广义上下文，替代传统从Transformer激活值中学习领域特定上下文的方式。具体而言，源域的key投影权重与目标域的query投影权重交互，实现跨域注意力迁移，以数据无关（data-agnostic）的方式进行知识迁移。

Result: 在10对源-目标不相交数据集的实验中，CATTLE仅从单一源数据集即可学习到广义上下文，在平均排名（2.9）和平均AUROC增益（3.7%）上均显著优于9个SOTA基线，这些基线涵盖机器学习、深度学习及基于大规模预训练模型的迁移学习等方法。代码已公开。

Conclusion: CATTLE证明了通过Transformer的投影权重而非激活值来捕获跨域规则级通用知识的可行性，有效解除了表格数据迁移学习中对共享特征的限制，为表格数据领域的知识迁移提供了一种数据无关且统计显著更优的新范式。

Abstract: Unlike images and text, applying transfer learning to tabular data is challenging due to heterogeneity in feature types, structures, and semantics across disparate domains. Existing methods assume shared features across data tables to enable knowledge transfer between domains, which is unrealistic in practice. \mds{This paper introduces generalized context learning to remove the requirement of shared features across domains. The generalized context captured by transformer projection weights for $key$, $value$, and $query$ provides rule-based generalization rather than the domain-specific context conventionally learned from transformer activations. Projection weights for $key$ from the source domain interact with the weight for $query$ in the target domain to achieve Cross-domain Attention Transfer Learning (CATTLE) in a data-agnostic manner. Our experiments on ten pairs of disjoint source-target data sets show that CATTLE can learn generalized context from a single source data set and is rank-wise and statistically superior to nine state-of-the-art baselines, including machine learning, deep learning, and transfer learning methods using large-scale pre-trained models. CATTLE achieves the best average rank (2.9) and delivers a 3.7% average AUROC gain over the baseline methods.} The CATTLE source code is available at https://tinyurl.com/pr5s8ywn.

</details>


### [207] [D-TAIA: Domain-Aware LLM Adaptation for Multi-Task Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28236)
*Sjoerd van Straten,Christine Jacob,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D-TAIA框架，通过参数高效微调基础模型（FM），结合域感知三元组损失预训练、FAISS最近邻检索和TAIA推理策略，解决预测性流程监控中数据稀缺、高流程熵和分布漂移三大挑战，实现下一步活动和剩余时间的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性流程监控（PPM）方法在数据稀缺、高流程熵和分布漂移三种实际条件下性能退化严重。虽然基础模型（尤其是大语言模型）提供了新的范式，但如何将其适配到多任务PPM并应对上述条件仍是开放挑战。现有基于FM的方法要么缺乏处理分布漂移的机制，要么依赖与连续时间预测任务结构不对齐的回归头。

Method: D-TAIA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 域感知三元组损失（DATL）预训练，用于增强模型对领域特征的表示能力；(2) FAISS最近邻检索用于剩余时间预测，避免直接使用回归头导致的机构错配问题；(3) TAIA推理策略，在微调过程中保留基础模型预训练的序列推理能力。整个框架仅对FM主干进行参数高效微调，使用约1000万参数的骨干网络。

Result: 在四个真实世界事件日志上的评估显示，D-TAIA在联合下一步活动和剩余时间预测任务上持续达到SOTA或与微调LLM和循环神经网络基线相当的性能。消融研究证实NLP和计算机视觉中的技术可以有效地迁移到PPM任务中，尽管各组件的贡献因数据集熵而异。

Conclusion: D-TAIA验证了将NLP和CV领域的技术迁移到PPM的有效性，以仅1000万参数的轻量级骨干实现了与大型模型和传统基线相当甚至更优的性能，为在资源受限条件下将基础模型应用于流程监控提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) enables organizations to forecast future process behavior, such as the next activity and remaining time of ongoing cases. In practice, three conditions cause existing methods to degrade, namely data scarcity, high process entropy and distributional shift. While Foundation Models (FMs), especially Large Language Models (LLMs), offer a new paradigm through broad sequential reasoning, adapting them to multi-task PPM under these conditions remains an open challenge. Existing FM-based approaches either lack mechanisms for handling distributional shift or rely on direct regression heads that can be structurally misaligned with continuous time prediction tasks. This paper introduces D-TAIA (Domain-aware Training and Attention-based Inference Architecture), a framework for a joint next activity and remaining time prediction task via parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an FM backbone. Our approach combines domain-aware triplet loss (DATL) pre-training with FAISS-based nearest neighbor retrieval for remaining time prediction, and adopts the TAIA inference strategy to preserve pre-trained sequential reasoning during fine-tuning. Evaluated across four real-world event logs, D-TAIA consistently shows SOTA or competitive performance compared to a fine-tuned LLM and a recurrent neural network baseline. Ablation studies confirm that techniques from NLP and computer vision can be transferred effectively to PPM with only a 10M-parameter backbone, though component contributions vary by dataset entropy.

</details>


### [208] [Efficient Online Continual Foundation Model Fine-Tuning for Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28237)
*Sjoerd van Straten,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个面向预测式流程监控（PPM）的在线持续微调基础模型（FM）框架，通过损失平台漂移检测自主识别事件流中的任务边界，并维护包含预训练方向与任务特定方向的统一知识子空间，在九个事件流上优于多个SOTA非FM方法及更新策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPM模型在动态环境下面临概念漂移问题，虽然已有工作转向在线持续学习，但这些方法从头训练紧凑的任务特定网络，存在持续的冷启动问题。基础模型虽有潜力解决冷启动，但如何在流程挖掘领域对其进行持续微调尚未被探索。

Method: COMPASS框架包含两大核心机制：(1) 基于损失平台（loss-plateau）的漂移检测模块，用于在事件流中自主识别任务边界，实现任务无关的持续学习；(2) 统一知识子空间，同时包含预训练方向和任务特定方向，在适应新任务的同时保留已有知识，避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在九个涵盖合成与真实世界概念漂移场景的事件流上、跨任务无关与任务感知两种设置、使用多个骨干网络并保持一致的超参数调优下，COMPASS在几乎所有评估中优于三个SOTA非FM竞争方法及两个更新策略基线，在具有周期性和复杂长运行案例的流上增益尤为显著，且计算开销相对非FM方法可接受。

Conclusion: COMPASS证明了基础模型在线持续微调在PPM领域的可行性，通过自适应子空间机制有效应对概念漂移与冷启动问题，为动态流程环境中的预测监控提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) models are increasingly deployed in dynamic environments where concept drift causes the underlying process distribution to shift over time. While recent work has moved toward online continual learning, existing methods train compact, task-specific networks entirely from scratch, leaving a persistent cold-start problem. Foundation Models (FMs) offer a compelling solution to this problem, but their continual fine-tuning in the process mining domain remains unexplored. We propose COMPASS (Continual Online foundation Model-based PPM with Adaptive SubSpaces), the first framework for online continual fine-tuning of FMs for PPM. COMPASS adapts loss-plateau drift detection to autonomously identify task boundaries in event streams and maintains a unified knowledge subspace including both pre-trained and task-specific directions. We evaluate our approach on nine event streams covering synthetic and real-world concept drift scenarios, across task-free and task-aware settings with multiple backbones and with consistent hyperparameter tuning across all methods. Our approach outperforms three SOTA non-FM competitors and two update strategy baselines, with particularly strong gains on streams exhibiting recurrent drift and complex, long-running cases, while incurring acceptable computational overhead compared to the non-FM competitors.

</details>


### [209] [Spectral Features Dominate BCG Respiratory-Event Detection: A Large-Scale Patient-Independent Comparison of Feature Groups in Sleep Apnea Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28242)
*Israel Campero Jurado,Zoe Bousraou,Lara Benning,Sara Padilla Neira,Alexander Breuss,Robert Riener,Esther Irene Schwarz,Elisabeth Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于512传感器电容压力垫和呼吸多导睡眠监测仪，对155例阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停患者进行了非接触式心冲击图（BCG）呼吸事件检测的全面特征比较研究。在严格的留一患者交叉验证下，随机森林和直方图梯度提升模型分别达到0.967和0.969的AUC-ROC。特征重要性分析表明频域特征占主导地位（呼吸频带功率占30.3%信息量），时域特征为主要补充（21.5%），两者合计贡献67%的判别信息，证明紧凑可解释的特征子集即可实现临床相关性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCG非接触式传感是长期睡眠呼吸暂停监测的潜在技术，但尚不清楚哪些信号特征对呼吸事件检测最具判别力。研究旨在通过文献指导的、患者无关的系统性比较，厘清不同特征组的相对贡献，为未来BCG系统的特征选择提供经验基础。

Method: 在155例住院评估OSA的患者（52女，103男）中，使用512传感器电容压力垫与呼吸多导睡眠监测仪同步记录。从6个空间不同信号通道提取特征，构建包含统计、时域、频域、小波、帧能量和非线性复杂度描述符的191维特征向量。采用严格的逐患者留出法交叉验证，使用随机森林和直方图梯度提升进行二分类（呼吸事件窗口vs无事件参考窗口），并进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 随机森林和直方图梯度提升分别达到AUC-ROC 0.967/0.969和AUC-PR 0.977/0.979。频域特征主导判别：0.1-0.4 Hz呼吸频带功率占全部判别信息的30.3%，FFT谱形状描述符贡献15.1%。AUC和曲线长度等时域特征提供21.5%的互补信息，小波和非线性特征仅贡献10.4%（59个特征）。频域+时域合计占67%判别信息。

Conclusion: 频域和时域特征的组合即可在患者无关验证下达到临床相关检测性能，证明紧凑、可解释的特征子集足以支撑BCG睡眠呼吸暂停监测系统，为未来系统的特征选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Unobtrusive ballistocardiographic (BCG) sensing is a promising modality for long-term sleep-apnea monitoring, yet it remains unclear which signal features are most discriminative for respiratory-event detection. We present a literature-guided, patient-independent comparison of ten BCG feature groups using a 512-sensor capacitive pressure mat recorded simultaneously with respiratory polygraphy in 155 patients (52 female, 103 male) undergoing in-hospital evaluation for obstructive sleep apnea. Features were extracted from six spatially distinct signal channels, yielding a 191-dimensional feature vector spanning general statistical, time-domain, frequency-domain, wavelet, frame-energy, and nonlinear complexity descriptors. Under strict leave-one-patient-out cross-validation for binary classification of respiratory-event windows versus event-free reference windows, Random Forest and Histogram Gradient Boosting achieved AUC-ROC of 0.967 and 0.969 and AUC-PR of 0.977 and 0.979, respectively. Feature-importance analysis revealed that frequency-domain features dominate discrimination: breathing-band power in the 0.1-0.4 Hz range accounted for 30.3% of total discriminative information across all spatial channels, and Fast Fourier Transform spectral-shape descriptors of the adaptively preprocessed channel contributed a further 15.1%. AUC and curve-length features provided the main complementary time-domain evidence (21.5%), whereas wavelet-derived and nonlinear features contributed smaller secondary effects (10.4% combined across 59 features). Frequency-domain and time-domain features together accounted for 67% of total discriminative information, demonstrating that a compact, interpretable subset of the full feature library achieves clinically relevant performance under patient-independent validation and providing an empirical basis for feature selection in future BCG systems.

</details>


### [210] [SinkSLOT: Sinkhorn via Sparse Lifted Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28262)
*Ian Hsieh,Soumya Snigdha Kundu,Tom Vercauteren,Reuben Dorent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SinkSLOT方法，通过期望切片提升传输计划来稀疏化Gibbs核，解决熵最优传输中Sinkhorn-Knopp算法的两大局限：每迭代O(N^2)的计算复杂度以及独立耦合作为参考度量导致的质量分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Sinkhorn-Knopp算法在处理大规模离散测度（N≥10^4）时计算代价过高（每迭代O(N^2)），且使用独立耦合作为正则化参考度量，在中等正则化强度下会将质量分配给高成本传输边。

Method: 提出SinkSLOT方法，将期望切片提升传输计划作为稀疏化Gibbs核的方式，采用非独立的先验耦合。理论证明其收敛性，并推导出使用L个切片时每轮稀疏Sinkhorn迭代复杂度为O(LN)。

Result: 理论证明确保收敛性，计算复杂度降至O(LN)；在合成基准实验中，相较现有最先进的稠密和稀疏EOT方法获得显著加速；在梯度流实验中验证了所提散度的实用性。

Conclusion: SinkSLOT有效解决了熵最优传输的可扩展性问题，同时避免了独立耦合参考度量的缺陷，产生的目标函数是不需要去偏的散度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Entropic optimal transport (EOT) has been shown to offer a computationally tractable approximation to exact optimal transport. However, the standard Sinkhorn-Knopp algorithm has two main limitations. First, given discrete measures with $N$ points, each iteration requires $O(N^2)$ operations, which restricts its use on large-scale datasets (e.g. $N\geq10^4$). Second, it uses the independent coupling as a reference measure for regularisation. This assigns mass to high-cost transport edges at moderate regularisation strengths. We propose SinkSLOT, which addresses both limitations by putting forth the expected sliced lifted transport plan as a natural way to sparsify the Gibbs kernel with a non-independent prior coupling. We prove that: 1) SinkSLOT converges; 2) with $L$ slices, each resulting sparse Sinkhorn iteration costs $O(LN)$; and 3) the resulting objective is a divergence requiring no debiasing. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks show that SinkSLOT delivers substantial speedups over state-of-the-art dense and sparse EOT methods. We also demonstrate the applicability of the proposed divergence in a gradient flow experiment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cai4cai/SinkSLOT.

</details>


### [211] [Residual-Guided Randomized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28267)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差引导的贪心构造方法，用于改进随机神经网络（如随机向量功能连接网络、极端学习机等）中隐藏层单元的单次随机采样问题。通过在每个阶段生成候选单元池、基于岭回归目标函数的精确残差减少量进行评分、选择Top-k单元并闭式重拟合输出权重，实现训练目标单调下降的渐进式训练。该方法在71个UCI基准数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络通过随机固定输入到隐藏层的参数并以闭式求解输出权重来获得快速且可解析的训练，但其性能高度依赖于一次性的、任务无关的隐藏单元随机采样。这种一次性采样往往导致冗余表示和模型容量利用不足，限制了随机神经网络的表达能力和泛化性能。

Method: 提出一种模型无关的残差引导贪心构造流程：(i) 生成随机候选单元池；(ii) 依据每个候选单元在岭正则化目标函数中引起的精确残差减少量进行评分；(iii) 选择Top-k个单元；(iv) 使用带直接输入链接的标准设计矩阵闭式重拟合输出层。整个过程迭代进行，保证训练目标单调递减。该方法的候选生成部分特定于具体架构，而评分-选择-重拟合循环对所有模型通用。

Result: 在UCI仓库的71个基准数据集上进行了广泛的实验，覆盖二分类和多分类任务。结果表明，所提出的残差引导模型在准确率、稳定性和整体排名性能方面一致优于相应的基线随机神经网络。

Conclusion: 该残差引导的贪心构造方法有效克服了随机神经网络一次性随机采样隐藏单元带来的冗余和容量利用不足问题，通过闭式残差减少准则实现了任务自适应的隐藏层构造，且模型无关的设计使其具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Randomized neural networks enable fast and analytically tractable training by fixing the input to hidden layer parameters at random and learning the output weights in closed form; however, their performance critically depends on a single uninformed draw of hidden units. This one shot and task uninformed feature construction often leads to redundant representations and suboptimal utilization of model capacity. To address this limitation, we propose a simple and broadly applicable residual guided procedure that greedily constructs the hidden layer using a closed form residual decrease criterion. At each stage, we (i) generate a pool of random candidate units, (ii) score each candidate by the exact reduction it induces in the ridge regularized objective, (iii) select the top k units, and (iv) refit the readout in closed form using the standard design with direct input links. This procedure yields a progressive training process with a guaranteed monotonic decrease of the training objective. The method is model agnostic: only the candidate generation is architecture specific, while the scoring selection refitting loop is shared across models. Extensive experiments on 71 benchmark datasets from the UCI repository, covering both binary and multiclass classification tasks, demonstrate that the proposed residual-guided models consistently outperform their baseline counterparts in terms of accuracy, stability, and overall ranking performance.

</details>


### [212] [Learning to Transfer Across Modes: Towards Unified Urban Mobility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28273)
*Yixuan Zhao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TransMod，一个统一框架，通过跨异质交通模式的迁移学习实现城市多模式出行需求预测，解决了空间结构不匹配和源模式数据丰富而目标模式数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统包含多种共存且相互依赖的出行模式，但现有预测方法大多针对单一模式开发，且隐含假设源和目标任务具有兼容的空间结构，这严重限制了在多模式场景下的适用性。此外，新兴模式的历史数据有限，使得跨模式的联合需求预测极具挑战性。

Method: TransMod首先构建一个共享的区域级空间表示，将不同空间粒度的移动系统对齐到公共空间，以缓解结构不匹配和分布偏移；然后基于该统一表示，学习从数据丰富的源模式到数据稀缺的目标模式的可迁移时空模式，减少对目标域历史数据的依赖。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，TransMod在预测性能上持续优于现有方法，并在目标数据有限的情况下表现出稳健的预测能力。

Conclusion: TransMod通过共享空间表示和跨模式知识迁移，有效解决了多模式出行需求预测中的空间异质性和数据稀缺性问题，为城市交通多模式联合预测提供了统一且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban transportation systems consist of multiple mobility modes that coexist within the same city and exhibit complex interdependencies, leading to correlated demand dynamics across modes. However, forecasting demand jointly across different modes remains challenging due to substantial heterogeneity in space and the limited availability of historical data for emerging modes. Existing forecasting methods are largely developed for individual mobility modes and implicitly assume compatible spatial structures between source and target systems, which severely restricts their applicability in multi-modal settings. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{TransMod}, a unified framework for urban mobility demand forecasting that enables effective knowledge transfer across heterogeneous mobility modes. TransMod constructs a shared zone-level spatial representation that aligns mobility systems with different spatial granularities into a common space, thereby reducing structural mismatch and distributional shift. Built on this unified representation, TransMod further learns transferable spatio-temporal patterns from data-rich source modes and adapts them to data-scarce target modes, alleviating the dependence on extensive target-domain histories. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TransMod consistently outperforms existing approaches and provides robust forecasting performance under limited target data.

</details>


### [213] [An algebraic proof of Colombo's difference-power determinant conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28274)
*Kun Li,Li Tie,Peng Wang,Zihan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Colombo在1928年提出的猜想：对于n为偶数、坐标两两不同的n维向量λ，差幂矩阵A_d(λ)对所有d≥n-1均非奇异。特别地，填补了超临界奇指数d≥n+1这一最后未解决的情形，从而完成了该猜想，并得出rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}的完整结论。证明方法是利用反证法，将假设的核向量转化为一个实的二元形式，其（含重数的）射影实线性因子数目超过其实Waring长度所允许的上界，从而导出矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colombo于1928年证明了差幂矩阵在d=n-1时的行列式非零（且为正），以及0≤d<n-1时秩为d+1，并提出“对所有d≥n-1行列式均非零”的猜想。此前已有结果覆盖了偶数d的情形，剩余的关键未解情况正是超临界的奇数指数d≥n+1，完成这一证明即完成了整个猜想，给出差幂矩阵秩的完整刻画。

Method: 证明采用反证法：假设存在d≥n+1（奇数）使得A_d(λ)奇异，则存在非零核向量。通过代数构造将该核向量转化为一个实的二元形式，利用差幂矩阵的特殊结构（λ坐标两两不同且n为偶数），分析该二元形式的射影实线性因子数目（含重数），使其超过该形式实Waring长度所允许的最大因子数，从而引出矛盾，证明核向量不存在。

Result: 完成了Colombo猜想：对所有d≥n-1，det A_d(λ)≠0。结合已有结果，完整得到rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}对一切d∈N成立。该结果统一涵盖了Colombo原本证明的低秩情形、后来已知的偶数d情形，以及此前悬而未决的奇数超临界指数情形。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了沉寂近一个世纪的Colombo猜想，给出了差幂矩阵在所有正整数指数下的完整秩公式。证明方法本身（将矩阵奇异性问题转化为二元形式实Waring分解的约束）也为同类矩阵问题提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be even, let $λ=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\in\mathbb{R}^n$ have pairwise distinct coordinates, and define the difference-power matrix \[ A_d(λ) := \bigl[(λ_r-λ_s)^d\bigr]_{r,s=1}^n, \qquad d\in\mathbb{N}. \] In 1928, Colombo proved that $\det A_{n-1}(λ)\ne0$---and hence $\det A_{n-1}(λ)>0$---and that $\operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=d+1$ for $0\le d<n-1$. He conjectured that \[ \det A_d(λ)\ne0 \qquad\text{for every } d\ge n-1. \] For even $d$, the conjectured nonsingularity follows from previously published results on distance-power matrices. The remaining open cases were therefore the supercritical odd exponents $d\ge n+1$. We prove nonsingularity for all these odd exponents, thereby completing Colombo's conjecture. Consequently, \[ \operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=\min\{n,d+1\} \qquad(d\in\mathbb{N}). \] Our proof converts a hypothetical kernel vector into a real binary form having more projective real linear factors, counted with multiplicity, than its real Waring length permits.

</details>


### [214] [Parser States Already Know: Structure-Conditioned KV Persistence for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28276)
*Linze Wu,Xinrui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PASK（Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence），一种利用解析器结构信号指导KV缓存压缩的方法，在0.33的KV预算下超越最强压缩基线17.39个百分点，并实现高达2.2倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化生成（JSON、SQL、函数调用）中单个字段错误即导致下游失败，而现有KV压缩方法未利用解析器转换过程中暴露的结构性信号（如必填字段、参数、结构边界等），导致任务关键信息在压缩中被丢失。

Method: PASK将解析器派生的结构信息转化为按层组（layer-group-specific）的KV持久化决策，具体包括：1) 利用任务错误敏感性设置最低保护层（minimum protection floors）；2) 利用注意力输出失真分配剩余KV容量；3) 离线校准阶段将信号编译为持久化策略，在线仅需轻量级结构条件查询。

Result: 在0.33总KV预算下，PASK在Qwen3-4B上跨越BFCL八个非实时和实时子类别平均超越最强压缩基线17.39个百分点；端到端服务中实现高达2.2倍吞吐量提升、3.3倍TPOT降低，峰值GPU内存仅为Full KV的0.53倍。

Conclusion: 通过将解析器结构信号与KV压缩策略有效结合，PASK填补了模型侧KV敏感性与任务级结构化风险之间的鸿沟，在不牺牲可靠性的前提下显著提升了结构化生成任务的效率与吞吐量。

Abstract: Structured generation underpins large language model (LLM) agents that produce JSON, SQL, and function calls, where a single wrong field can cause the downstream action to fail. Constrained decoding already tracks parser transitions to enforce formal validity, and these transitions expose how generated tokens participate in schema-critical decisions such as required fields, arguments, and structural boundaries under the active grammar. Existing KV compression largely leaves this task-relevant structural signal unused. We introduce PASK (Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence), which turns parser-derived structure into layer-group-specific KV persistence decisions. PASK addresses the mismatch between model-side KV sensitivity and task-level structured risk by using task-error sensitivity to set minimum protection floors and attention-output distortion to allocate residual KV capacity. An offline calibration stage compiles these signals into a persistence policy, leaving only lightweight structure-conditioned lookup online. At a targe total KV budget of 0.33, PASK outperforms the strongest compressed baseline by 17.39 percentage points on average across eight BFCL non-live and Live subcategories on Qwen3-4B. In end-to-end serving, PASK achieves up to 2.2x higher throughput and 3.3x lower TPOT, while using 0.53x the peak GPU memory of Full KV.

</details>


### [215] [VISTA: Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28306)
*Zewen Ding,Zezhong Wu,Zhou Tao,Shida Wang,Shizhuo Hou,YongXiang Hua,Haoyu Cao,Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VISTA是一种改进的on-policy self-distillation方法，通过利用outcome-verified rollouts将教师分布自适应地调整到学生分布，解决了标准OPSD中固定教师分布可能误导学生推理的问题，在AIME和HMMT基准上相较OPSD取得了0.6-2.1个点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD将教师分布视为固定目标，仅更新学生，但privileged conditioning不能保证教师始终为problem-only推理提供最合适的目标。当教师分布与学生有效推理不对齐时，这种单向监督会误导学生。因此需要一种机制让教师适应学生的分布。

Method: VISTA保留标准OPSD的学生更新，同时使用outcome-verified rollouts（即结果验证通过的学生推理轨迹）来适应教师分布。在每个验证通过的rollout中，VISTA仅限制在教师-学生KL散度最大的top-k位置上进行教师适应。VISTA复用OPSD的rollout和损失函数，不引入额外采样或独立奖励目标。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25基准上，使用Qwen3模型（1.7B、4B、8B），VISTA在所有规模上均取得最高的Avg@12，相较OPSD提升了0.6、0.7和2.1个点。

Conclusion: 结果验证了从outcome-verified rollouts中获取的学生监督信号的价值，并表明student-to-teacher adaptation是OPSD一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a problem-only student on its own rollouts using dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher that also sees a reference solution. However, standard OPSD treats the teacher distribution as a fixed target along the student's rollout and updates only the student %, although -- even though privileged conditioning does not guarantee that the teacher always provides the most appropriate target for problem-only reasoning. This one-way supervision can therefore misdirect the student when the teacher distribution is misaligned with valid student reasoning. We therefore introduce Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation (VISTA), which preserves the standard OPSD student update while using outcome-verified rollouts to adapt the teacher toward the student distribution. Within each verified rollout, VISTA further restricts this adaptation to the top-$k$ positions with the largest teacher--student KL divergence. Notably, VISTA reuses the rollout and loss function from standard OPSD, introducing no additional sampling or separate reward objective. Across AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25 with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, VISTA achieves the highest Avg@12 at every scale, improving over OPSD by $0.6$, $0.7$, and $2.1$ points, respectively. These results demonstrate the value of student supervision from outcome-verified rollouts and highlight student-to-teacher adaptation as a promising direction for OPSD.

</details>


### [216] [Deriving Scaling Laws for OpenEuroLLM Models: Learning Rate, Batch Size and Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28308)
*Niccolò Ajroldi,Diana Alexandra Onutu,Haider Al-Tahan,Jörg Franke,Sampo Pyysalo,Jenia Jitsev,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了预训练密集大型语言模型中学习率和批大小的缩放行为，包括联合最优超参数的边际演化规律、Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度下的退火收益、最优超参数在稳定与衰减阶段的迁移性，以及损失对模型容量和数据集规模的依赖关系，并开源了全部预训练运行数据，为OpenEuroLLM模型的开发奠定了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型预训练规模的持续扩大，理解学习率和批大小等关键超参数如何随模型容量和数据规模缩放至关重要。然而现有研究主要关注联合最优超参数的缩放，对超参数随模型规模和数据规模的边际演化规律、学习率退火阶段的收益、以及超参数在不同训练阶段间的迁移性等问题缺乏系统性研究，该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 该研究在英语主导语料上预训练多种规模的密集大语言模型，采用Warmup-Stable-Decay（WSD）学习率调度。具体包括：(1) 研究联合最优学习率和批大小的缩放行为及其随模型容量和数据规模的边际演化规律，并建立相应模型；(2) 在广泛的超参数设置、模型规模和数据预算下系统评估学习率退火带来的收益；(3) 考察最优学习率和批大小在稳定阶段与衰减阶段之间的迁移性；(4) 评估近期提出的显式建模模型容量与数据集规模交互作用的缩放律形式，检验其对欠训练和过训练区域的拟合能力。

Result: 研究发现(WSD)调度下学习率退火能在广泛的超参数设置下带来一致的收益；近期提出的显式建模模型容量与数据集规模交互的缩放律形式在捕获欠训练和过训练区域方面表现尤为有效；最优学习率和批大小在稳定阶段与衰减阶段之间存在可研究的关系。该研究为OpenEuroLLM模型的开发建立了首个基线和缩放流程，并开源了全部预训练运行数据。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了大语言模型预训练中超参数缩放的实证基线，验证了显式交互建模的缩放律在欠训练/过训练场景中的有效性，并通过开源运行数据为后续OpenEuroLLM模型及其他大规模预训练研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: We study the scaling behavior of learning rate and batch size in pretraining dense large language models on English-prevalent corpora. Beyond scaling \textit{jointly optimal} learning rates and batch sizes, we investigate their \textit{marginal} evolution with model capacity and data scale and develop a model that captures these relationships. As we employ a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning rate schedule, we further investigate the gains from learning rate annealing over a broad range of hyperparameters settings, models and data budgets, and whether the optimal learning rate and batch size \textit{transfer} between the stable and decay phases. Finally, we characterize the dependence of loss on model capacity and dataset size, evaluating recently proposed scaling forms that explicitly model their interaction. We find these approaches particularly effective at capturing both undertraining and overtraining regimes across our experiments. This study establishes a first baseline and scaling procedure for the development of future OpenEuroLLM models. We open-source the complete collection of pretraining runs used in this study.

</details>


### [217] [SymboLLM-FE: LLM-Accelerated Symbolic Regression for Automated Feature Engineering on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28408)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Zi-Yi Jia,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymboLLM-FE，将符号回归与大型语言模型相结合用于自动化特征工程，解决了传统AutoFE可解释性差和LLM-based AutoFE需要多次迭代且存在偏见/幻觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据常因特征信息量不足而缺乏判别力，AutoFE可自动生成和选择特征以提升模型性能和效率，但传统AutoFE依赖盲目数学变换导致特征可解释性差，而LLM-based AutoFE需要昂贵的多轮迭代，且存在偏见和幻觉风险。

Method: 通过符号回归提取与目标强相关且具有数学表达能力的公式以增强模型性能，再利用具备丰富先验知识的LLM对这些公式进行细化以确保可解释性。采用基于统计先验的LLM细化机制和个位数级别的LLM调用次数。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集和四个Kaggle竞赛上的实验结果表明，SymboLLM-FE优于现有AutoFE方法。

Conclusion: SymboLLM-FE同时解决了可解释性差和迭代次数多两大挑战，通过统计先验引导的LLM细化机制和极少的LLM调用实现了高效且可解释的自动化特征工程。

Abstract: Tabular data, as a core data format in machine learning, often lacks the discriminative power needed for high-performance modeling due to insufficient feature informativeness. Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) overcomes this by automating feature generation and selection, ensuring both model performance and operational efficiency. However, traditional AutoFE often yield features with poor interpretability because they rely on blind mathematical transformations, while large language models (LLM)-based AutoFE faces challenges in requiring costly multi-round iterations to generate high-utility features to effectively enhance model performance, compounded by inherent risks of bias and hallucination. In this paper, we combine symbolic regression with LLMs for feature engineering (SymboLLM-FE) to solve these challenges. We extract mathematically expressive formulas strongly correlated with the target via symbolic regression, which can enhance model performance, then refine them by LLMs with rich prior knowledge to ensure interpretability. Empirical results on six real-world datasets and four Kaggle competitions demonstrate that SymboLLM-FE outperforms existing AutoFE. SymboLLM-FE also addresses the dual challenges of poor interpretability and numerous iterations by employing a statistical prior-grounded LLM refinement mechanism and single-digit LLM calls.

</details>


### [218] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），通过将谱核参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，解决FNO在不同周期域之间不可迁移的问题，并在热方程和材料科学任务中验证了其跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO虽然在训练和评估时与网格分辨率无关，但其离散谱权重以整数傅里叶模态号索引，对应特定的物理波矢量。当应用到不同周期域时，相同的训练权重作用于不同波矢量，导致FNO静默地表示了一个不同的算子，使其无法用于需要跨域迁移的任务。

Method: 提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），将谱核参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，从而使算子在不同形状和大小的周期域上保持一致的行为，实现域无关的操作。

Result: 在简单热方程和一个实际的材料科学任务（学习不同晶体结构间的交换关联势）上验证了EFNO，结果表明EFNO能够泛化到未见过的网格尺寸和域。

Conclusion: EFNO作为一种域无关的FNO替代方案，有效解决了FNO在跨域迁移时的局限性，能够在不同周期域上一致地学习算子。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>


### [219] [Curvature-Conditioned Multiscale Momentum with Sphere Constraints for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28442)
*Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Mingze Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种曲率条件化的多尺度动量方法，仅在平坦方向（小特征值特征方向）应用慢衰减与快衰减动量组合，并配合球面约束防止参数膨胀，从而加速LLM预训练，在多种架构和模型规模上显著加速Muon优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中计算成本高昂，且损失景观病态条件数严重。现有自适应优化器（如AdamW、Muon）依赖梯度归一化，对病态曲率的缓解有限，平坦方向（小特征值方向）的进展缓慢，而该方向主导最终损失下降，亟需针对性加速策略。

Method: 提出曲率条件化的多尺度动量方法：仅在平坦方向应用多尺度动量，包含慢衰减分量（用于噪声抑制）和快衰减分量（用于快速曲率适应）；同时引入球面约束技术防止朴素组合导致的参数膨胀和有效学习率过快衰减。在理论上验证了加速度效应并揭示了平坦方向多尺度动量的设计原则。

Result: 在多种架构（稠密、MoE）和模型规模（0.12B-2.3B参数）上进行实验，证明该方法显著加速了Muon优化器的预训练收敛。

Conclusion: 平坦方向的多尺度动量能够有效加速LLM预训练，球面约束是防止参数膨胀的关键技术，理论分析验证了方法的有效性并提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Pretraining accounts for a large fraction of the total computational cost in LLM training. However, noise-dominant gradients and the highly ill-conditioned loss landscape bring severe challenges. Although modern adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Muon have achieved great success in large-scale pretraining, their reliance on gradient normalization offers limited mitigation of the ill-conditioned curvature. The progress along flat directions (eigen-directions of small eigenvalues), which dominates the final loss reduction, remains relatively slow. To enhance training dynamics along flat directions, we propose a curvature-conditioned multiscale momentum method with sphere constraints, delivering steady acceleration in LLM pretraining. This multiscale momentum, applied only along flat directions, pairs a slow-decay component for noise reduction with a fast-decay component for rapid curvature adaptation, harnessing their complementary strengths. Crucially, we employ a sphere constraint technique to prevent parameter inflation and excessively rapid effective learning rate decay that would otherwise arise from a naive combination. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method significantly accelerates Muon across diverse architectures (dense, MoE) and model sizes (0.12B--2.3B parameters). Theoretically, we verify the acceleration effect and provide insight into the design principles underlying the flat-direction multiscale momentum.

</details>


### [220] [How Proper Scoring Rules Shape LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28482)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim,Ville A. Satopää,Philip E. Tetlock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种合适的评分规则（proper scoring rules）作为LLM预测器训练目标时的效果差异。尽管这些规则理论上都鼓励真实概率报告，但训练出的模型在校准、概率使用以及偏差-信息-噪声画像上存在显著差异，聚合准确率和判别力差异较小。Brier训练的模型Brier分数最低且AUC-ROC最高，而log训练的模型log分数最高且校准误差最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奖励函数选择如何影响LLM预测器的性能和行为。五种评分规则理论上具有相同的诚实概率报告激励，但实践中可能因优化动态不同而产生不同效果，需要实证检验。

Method: 对已解决的真实世界事件的二元预测任务，比较五种合适的评分规则作为训练目标；从校准性、概率使用、偏差-信息-噪声分解、聚合准确率和判别力等多个维度评估训练后的LLM预测器。

Result: 不同评分规则训练出的模型在多个维度上表现不同：Brier训练的模型有最低Brier分数和最高AUC-ROC；log训练的模型有最高log分数和最低校准误差；聚合性能相似的模型也可能通过不同的偏差-信息-噪声组合达到相同性能；各条件使用单一种子，部分差异可能源于训练随机性。

Conclusion: 合适的评分规则在作为训练目标时并不能互换使用。奖励选择不仅影响LLM的预测表现，还影响其预测误差的结构性分布。

Abstract: This paper evaluates how reward function choice shapes the performance and behavior of LLM forecasters. We compare five proper scoring rules as training objectives for binary forecasts of resolved real-world events. Although the rules share the same theoretical incentive for truthful probability reporting, the resulting models differ in calibration, probability use, and estimated profiles of bias, information, and noise, with smaller differences in aggregate accuracy and discrimination. The Brier-trained model has the lowest observed Brier score and highest AUC-ROC, while the log-trained model has the highest observed log score and lowest calibration error. Models with similar aggregate performance also reach that performance through different combinations of bias, information, and noise. Proper scoring rules therefore need not behave interchangeably as training objectives. Reward choice may shape not only how well an LLM forecasts, but how its forecasting errors are structured. Each condition uses a single seed, so some differences may reflect training stochasticity.

</details>


### [221] [REPLICANT: Learning Policies for Evading and Hardening Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28499)
*Shae McFadden,Ilias Tsingenopoulos,Mario D'Onghia,Alexander Herzog,Myles Foley,Chris Hicks,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Fabio Pierazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Replicant，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，在严格标签只能黑盒威胁模型下学习可迁移的恶意软件逃避攻击策略，在七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上取得了78.8%的平均攻击成功率，比现有最先进方法相对提升20.9%-39.2%，同时用于对抗训练时能产生具有更强泛化鲁棒性的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的恶意软件检测在实际对抗场景中的鲁棒性评估不足。当前最先进的攻击方法通常假设攻击者能访问训练数据、特征空间或目标模型的置信度分数等特权信息，无法有效建模真实世界中的对抗者。因此需要一种更贴近现实的攻击框架来评估检测器的真实鲁棒性。

Method: Replicant是一个深度强化学习框架，在标签只能黑盒威胁模型下学习恶意软件逃避检测的策略。它通过强化学习学习两个关键能力：如何修改恶意软件样本以及何时查询目标检测器。该策略是可复用的，能够跨样本、跨检测器、跨特征空间迁移。

Result: 在七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上，Replicant达到了78.8%的平均攻击成功率，相对现有最先进方法有20.9%-39.2%的改进，且查询效率最高。用于对抗训练时，Replicant产生的检测器具有更泛化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 学习逃避任务不仅带来更强的攻击性能，更重要的是为加固恶意软件检测器提供了更好的信号。Replicant证明了在严格标签只能黑盒威胁模型下，通过深度学习实现高效、可迁移的逃避攻击是可行的，并为检测器的鲁棒性强化提供了更有价值的训练信号。

Abstract: To determine the real-world effectiveness of machine learning based malware detection, it is vital to evaluate its robustness against highly capable adversaries. However, state-of-the-art attacks do not effectively model realistic adversaries, as they often assume access to privileged information such as the training data, feature space, or confidence scores of the target. In this work, we present Replicant, a deep reinforcement learning framework that learns the realistic task of evasion under a strict label-only black-box threat model. Replicant learns a reusable policy on how to modify a malware sample and when to query the target, which transfers across samples, detectors, and feature spaces. Across seven Android malware detectors and three feature spaces, Replicant is the strongest and most query-efficient approach achieving a mean attack success rate of 78.8%, a relative improvement of 20.9%-39.2% over the state-of-the-art. Furthermore, when used for adversarial training, Replicant also outperforms the state-of-the art by producing detectors with more generalizable robustness. With Replicant we demonstrate that learning the task of evasion not only results in stronger attack performance but, crucially, provides a better signal for hardening malware detectors.

</details>


### [222] [An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28541)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究代码世界模型在采样门（sampling gate）接受下的可靠性边界问题。当遗漏模式为环形冻结态（annular freeze mode）包裹不可达内部区域时，被认证模型在可达查询集上可被精确确定，而超出可达范围则成为"规范自由度"（gauge）。作者在最小环形仪器上证明极端情形（错误拓扑的填充圆盘伪影无法被任何采样门证伪），并通过三个模型族的LLM合成实验，展示单个通道宽度γ参数如何使同一伪影经历三个区间：不可证伪且无害、可证伪且代价高昂、立即被证伪。三个原则组织实证：危险是相对于可达性的拓扑；修复受参数与传感器约束；缓解必须匹配错误的维度与方向。在n维空间中，壳层使误识别接近必然，而危险仍完全可利用，两条轴线相互独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样门接受的代码世界模型可能存在无法被采样检测到的系统性错误（特别是拓扑错误），这些错误在实际运行时可能造成不可预见的后果。需要从理论上刻画认证模型的认知边界与错误代价，并探索实用的检测与缓解策略。

Method: 采用门商（gate quotient）形式化工具将问题精确表述：确定性接受决定模型在可达查询集上的精确行为，超出可达范围则为规范自由度。在最小环形仪器上构造极端反例（错误拓扑的填充圆盘伪影），并通过三种LLM模型族进行合成实验，系统改变通道宽度γ观察伪影状态转移。使用持久同调（persistent homology）作为拓扑传感工具，设计维度匹配的持久栅栏（persisted fence）和双重自由证书（dual freedom certificate）等缓解技术。

Result: 被采样门通过的模型可"完全正确"于可达查询而"任意错误"于不可达区域。通道可利用时，盲模型的利用代价从1.09降至~0（在γ~0.1处出现拐点）；隐藏通道保持完整危险（1.12）。维度匹配的持久栅栏使利用代价从0.999降至0.058，双重自由证书对称地使失败从1.769降至0.029。模型能提出正确拓扑但无法确定参数，且提出的拓扑跟随传感摘要的错误beta_1而非真实值。在n维中壳层使误识别接近必然，但危险仍完全可利用。

Conclusion: 采样验证在拓扑意义上是"局部可靠、全局不可靠"的：可达集之外模型行为完全不受约束。危险的本质是相对于可达性的拓扑结构，而非绝对拓扑。修复既有参数边界又有传感器边界限制；缓解策略必须与错误的维度和方向匹配才能有效。拓扑错误检测与模型可靠性保障之间存在根本性的维度悖论：检测能力增强并不等于风险降低，两条轴线需要独立处理。

Abstract: A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. Third, mitigation must match the error's dimension and direction: point fences fail against the one-dimensional boundary, a dimension-matched persisted fence collapses exploitation to a two-lesson transient (0.999 to 0.058), and the dual freedom certificate collapses the invented-mode failure symmetrically (1.769 to 0.029). In n dimensions the shell makes misidentification near-certain while the danger stays fully exploitable: the two axes are independent.

</details>


### [223] [DARTS: Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28547)
*Aaryan Ajay Sharma,Sai Nishanth Padala,Seganrasan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTS是一种针对解码器模型模型合并后表示偏差的轻量级修正方法，通过熵加权的L1损失和逐位置加性偏置来解决因果注意力掩码带来的偏差累积和位置重要性不均的问题，在代码生成、数学推理和指令跟随任务上显著优于现有方法，仅增加0.1%的参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并是一种无需额外训练即可将多个任务微调的LLM合并为多任务模型的方法，但合并模型存在表示偏差问题。先前工作仅针对编码器模型研究了该偏差的修正，解码器模型因自回归特性尚未被研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，解决解码器模型中表示偏差的两个独特挑战：因果注意力掩码导致的偏差跨位置累积，以及不同token位置重要性不均（高熵位置远比低熵位置关键）。

Method: DARTS方法包含两个关键设计：(1) 熵加权L1损失，对高熵（决策关键）位置给予更大权重，优先修正这些对生成质量影响最大的位置的误差；(2) 逐位置加性偏置，用于捕捉位置依赖的偏差，同时避免过度参数化。该方法基于轻量级修正模块，仅增加约0.1%的总参数量。

Result: 在三个领域（代码生成HumanEval、数学推理GSM8K、指令跟随AlpacaEval）基于Llama-2-7B模型进行广泛评估，DARTS相比标准surgery方法取得了显著改进，且增加的参数量可忽略不计（占总参数0.1%）。

Conclusion: DARTS证明了在解码器模型中修正表示偏差是可行的且有效的，通过考虑解码器特有的位置依赖性和位置重要性不均衡性，该方法的修正效果优于传统手术方法，为多任务LLM合并提供了实用的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific fine-tuned LLMs into a single multi-task model without additional training. However, merged models are known to suffer from representation bias: systematic drift between the merged model's hidden states and those of each individual source model. Prior work (Yang et al., 2024a) study and mitigate this bias for encoder-based vision models using a lightweight correction module trained with L1 loss. However, such bias is not studied for decoder models due to their autoregressive nature. We analyze the problem of representation bias in decoder models, and show two challenges absent in encoders: (1) the causal attention mask causes bias to accumulate across token positions, requiring position-dependent correction; and (2) not all token positions are equally important, i.e., high-entropy (decision-critical) positions matter far more than low-entropy ones. To address these challenges, we propose Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS). DARTS employs a novel entropy-weighted L1 loss to upweight correction at high-entropy positions where errors most affect generation quality, and a per-position additive bias that captures position-dependent error without overparameterization. We perform extensive evaluation on three domains: code generation (HumanEval), mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), and instruction following (AlpacaEval) on Llama-2-7B models, and show DARTS achieves significant improvement over the standard surgery approach while adding negligible parameters ($0.1\%$ of total parameters).

</details>


### [224] [Advancing Interaction-Sensitive Feature Selection: Novel Relief-Based Algorithms, Expanded Comparisons, and Recommendations for Biomedical Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28552)
*Kia Kazemi-Nia,Harsh Bandhey,Philip J. Freda,Ryan J. Urbanowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对scikit-rebate Python包进行了重构、优化和扩展，引入了5种新的Relief-based特征选择算法变体（RBA），并在多样化的基因组模拟数据上进行了严格的基准比较，结果表明新算法在检测特征交互方面表现优异，且重构后运行时间缩短了10-35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维生物医学数据建模中，可靠的特征选择可以降低计算成本、提高建模性能并产生更简单可解释的模型。然而，大多数基于过滤器的特征选择方法难以检测特征交互，而包装器或嵌入式方法计算开销大。Relief-based算法（RBA）作为过滤器方法，既对特征交互敏感又能缓解上述限制，因此有必要对现有RBA变体进行系统化扩展和评估。

Method: 研究重构并扩展了scikit-rebate Python包，包含现有和新提出的RBA变体，包括SWRF*、mu-Relief以及5种新提出的RBA变体（采用替代的邻居选择和特征评分策略）。所有RBA在模拟基因组数据集上进行了评估，数据集在样本量、特征数量、遗传力和关联类型（主效应和交互效应）方面有所变化，比较了预测特征排名和运行时间。

Result: 除mu-Relief外，所有RBA都能有效检测噪声数据中的双向交互。使用'远'评分的RBA最擅长检测双向交互，其中MultiSWRFDB*表现最佳，但对主效应的敏感度较差。SWRF、MultiSWRF、MultiSURF和MultiSWRFDB在主效应和双向交互数据集上表现最佳，MultiSWRFDB在考虑三向交互时表现最好。重构后的运行时间减少了10-35倍。

Conclusion: 新引入的RBA算法是表现最强的算法之一，通过稳健地保留主效应和双向上位性交互，这些算法为下游建模保留了预测信号，验证了扩展scikit-rebate包的价值。

Abstract: As a precursor to high-dimensional biomedical data modeling, reliable feature selection can reduce computational expense, improve modeling performance, and yield simpler, more interpretable models. However, most filter-based feature selection methods struggle to detect feature interactions, while wrapper or embedded feature selection methods are computationally expensive. Relief-based algorithms (RBAs) are filter methods that are sensitive to feature interactions while mitigating these other limitations. This study (1) refactors, optimizes, and expands the scikit-rebate Python package with existing and newly proposed RBA variants and (2) conducts rigorous RBA benchmark comparisons across diverse genomic simulations. We expand scikit-rebate to include SWRF*, mu-Relief, and 5 novel RBA variants implementing alternative strategies for neighbor selection and feature scoring. All RBAs were evaluated to compare predictive feature ranking and runtime across simulated genomic datasets varying in sample size, number of features, heritability, and underlying association type (e.g. main effects and interactions). All RBAs, except mu-Relief, were proficient in detecting 2-way interactions in noisy data. RBAs utilizing 'far' scoring were best at detecting 2-way interactions - with MultiSWRFDB* top-performing - but were far less sensitive to main effects. SWRF, MultiSWRF, MultiSURF, and MultiSWRFDB yielded top performance across main effect and 2-way interaction datasets with MultiSWRFDB performing best when also considering 3-way interactions. Refactoring of scikit-rebate resulted in 10 to 35-fold reductions in RBA runtimes. The newly introduced RBAs were among the strongest performing, and by robustly retaining both main effects and 2-way epistatic interactions, these algorithms preserve predictive signals for downstream modeling.

</details>


### [225] [Blog: Survey of Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28557)
*Ruoran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文档是一篇2025-2026年神经网络优化方法的综述，将优化器与训练方法沿四个独立轴（时间估计、更新几何、视界管理、表示与系统）组织分析，并给出一个非胜利主义的结论：矩阵感知方法虽有实质进步，但不存在能普遍替代AdamW的万能优化器，优化器排名取决于多种上下文因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络优化领域的现状已不再只是Adam变体的简单迭代。设计空间已从坐标扩展到矩阵与层，从固定训练水平扩展到时域策略，从数学更新规则扩展到需兼容分片与低精度计算的状态表示。当前缺乏一个系统的框架来组织这些日益多样化的方法，使研究者难以对优化器优劣做出有依据的判断。

Method: 作者提出沿四个基本独立的轴来组织近期优化器与训练优化方法：时间估计（temporal estimation）、更新几何（update geometry）、视界管理（horizon management）以及表示与系统（representation and systems）。该框架将Muon的谱归一化、Shampoo和SOAP的历史矩阵统计、自适应与混合矩阵方法、内存高效优化器、免调度训练、小批量修正以及量化优化器状态等统一纳入分析。

Result: 核心经验性结论是审慎而非胜利主义的：矩阵感知方法代表真实进步，但没有任何与上下文无关的AdamW替代品。排名随模型规模、数据与参数比、批次大小、调度方式、参数分区、调优预算以及目标度量（如token数、FLOPs、墙钟时间或内存）的不同而变化。

Conclusion: 实际启示是应从组合视角看待优化器设计，并需要更严格的协议来评估优化器的相关论断。

Abstract: Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

</details>


### [226] [QGPINNs: A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Nonlocal Differential Equations on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28589)
*Vaibhav Mehandiratta,Saket Ramchandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了QGPINNs，一个基于PyTorch的物理信息神经网络框架，用于求解量子图上的非局部微分方程。该框架在每个边上用神经网络逼近解，通过统一的图基损失函数施加控制方程、初边值和顶点传输条件（包括连续性和Kirchhoff-Neumann条件），支持多阶分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程，并集成了软/硬约束、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入和可学习的奇异性捕获特征等图自适应学习策略，同时扩展到反问题识别。在基准图结构和真实网络（IEEE 14节点系统和农业排水网络）上验证了准确性、效率和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子图上的非局部微分方程（特别是分数阶方程）缺乏通用且高效的数值求解框架，传统方法难以处理复杂图结构上的多阶分数阶问题及顶点传输条件。作者旨在开发一个通用的物理信息神经网络计算框架，统一处理正问题和反问题。

Method: 1) 图每条边独立使用神经网络逼近解；2) 构建统一的图基损失函数，同时施加控制方程、初值、Dirichlet边界条件以及顶点处的连续性和Kirchhoff-Neumann传输条件；3) 针对两类模型：多阶分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程；4) 集成图自适应策略：软/硬约束施加、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入、可学习奇异性捕获特征；5) 扩展至反问题，识别分数阶算子的阶数和物理参数。

Result: 通过基准图结构和两个真实网络（IEEE 14节点电力系统、开放式农业排水网络）上的数值实验，验证了框架的准确性、计算效率和物理一致性，证明其在正问题和反问题中均有效。

Conclusion: QGPINNs为量子图上非局部微分方程提供了一种通用、可扩展的PINN求解框架，融合了图结构与物理信息，可有效处理弱奇异解和反问题，为复杂网络上的分数阶微分方程建模提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose QGPINNs, a physics-informed neural network framework developed in PyTorch for the numerical solution of nonlocal differential equations on quantum graphs. The framework is designed as a general computational implementation in which the solution on each edge of the graph is approximated by a neural network, while a unified graph-based loss function enforces the governing equations together with initial, boundary, and vertex transmission conditions. In particular, the formulation incorporates standard continuity and Kirchhoff-Neumann vertex conditions and Dirichlet boundary conditions into the learning process to couple the local edge-wise neural approximations into a global solution on the graph. The framework is developed for two representative classes of nonlinear models: multi-order fractional elliptic problems and time-fractional evolution equations on quantum graphs. To improve accuracy and training stability, QGPINNs integrates several graph-adapted learning strategies, including soft and hard constraint enforcement, dynamic loss balancing, Fourier feature embeddings, and a learnable singularity-capturing feature for weakly singular solutions arising in the considered problems. The framework also extends naturally to inverse problems, including the identification of the orders of fractional operators and physical parameters from noisy observational data. We validate the accuracy, computational efficiency, and physical consistency of the proposed framework through numerical experiments on benchmark graph structures and real-world networks, including the IEEE 14-bus system and an open-channel agricultural drainage network.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Time Capsule of Testable Human Knowledge: 41 Years of Jeopardy! in a Single Free Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27459)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让一个仅9GB的开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit）回答完整Jeopardy!题库（529,939道题目，覆盖1984-2025年41个季度），证明了曾经的"知识快照"——IBM Watson所代表的封闭、昂贵、不可移动的知识系统——如今已变得便携且几乎零成本。模型在严格强制回答协议下答对67.0%的题目，在训练截止后发布的题目上保持65%的正确率（Claude Opus 4.8达到95%），而Watson对此类题目得分为零，表明知识能力已不再被冻结在特定时间点上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比IBM Watson在2011年作为封闭、不可移动的巨型知识系统（依赖千文档语料库和POWER7集群）与如今开放权重模型的可移植性和开放性，探讨知识快照这一概念的演变，并首次在完整Jeopardy!题库上系统评估现代开放权重模型。

Method: 使用单个9GB的开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit量化）对完整开放Jeopardy!线索数据集（529,939条线索）进行推理，采用严格的强制回答协议，结合精确匹配和模糊匹配两种评分方式评估性能。同时将训练数据暴露视为两代系统共有的特征，并重点考察模型在训练截止日期之后播出的线索上的表现作为公平的泛化测试。

Result: 模型在全部529,939条线索上达到67.0%的准确率，在事实类类别上超过85%；针对训练截止后出现的线索，模型保持65%的准确率（Claude Opus 4.8达到95%），而Watson由于构建时的封闭性在这些新线索上得分为零。整个评估首次覆盖了完整41季Jeopardy!数据集。

Conclusion: 昔日Watson所象征的"文化可回答知识快照"如今可以从服务器机房迁移到几乎零成本的便携文件中，且不像Watson那样被冻结在其构建时刻。现代开放权重模型不仅在规模和成本上实现了数量级的缩小，还具备了跨时间泛化的能力，标志着知识获取民主化的重大转变。

Abstract: In 2011, IBM's Watson was something like a sealed capsule of its era's queryable knowledge. Its DeepQA system defeated the strongest human Jeopardy! champions, but the knowledge that let it do so lived in a curated billion-document corpus running on a cluster of POWER7 servers, frozen at build time and impossible to move or copy. We show that the same kind of artifact, a snapshot of what a culture can answer, is now portable and essentially free. We evaluate a single 9 GB open-weight model (Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit) against the complete open Jeopardy! clue dataset, 529,939 clues across all 41 broadcast seasons from 1984 to 2025. To our knowledge this is the first time a model has been run over the full corpus. The 41 years mark only how long the questions were collected. What they test is far older and broader: the accumulated body of human general knowledge a culture considers worth knowing, from ancient history and dead languages to science, literature, and geography, with a verified answer for every item. The model answers 67.0% of all clues under a strict forced-response protocol with exact and fuzzy matching, and exceeds 85% on factoid categories. We treat training-data exposure as something both systems share rather than a flaw unique to language models. Watson's case is in fact the more extreme one. Its corpus was assembled to contain Jeopardy answers and it was tuned on past clues, and it could not answer anything outside that curated distribution. The decisive test is whether a model can answer clues that did not exist when it was built. On clues aired after its training cutoff, the local model holds 65% and Claude Opus 4.8 holds 95%, while Watson by construction scores zero. The capability survives the move from a server room to a file you could seal in a time capsule, and unlike Watson it is not frozen to its own moment.

</details>


### [228] [Rating the Raters: Rasch Measurement Theory for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27463)
*Pratik S. Sachdeva,Nathan Boudol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将Rasch测量理论（RMT）应用于LLM评估的三个范式（LLM-as-examinee、LLM-as-judge、LLM-as-rater），并通过九种不同能力水平LLM在仇恨言论语料上的多面Rasch模型案例研究，揭示了LLM与人类评分者在严格度、题目校准、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性和评分量表使用等方面的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已同时担任被评估者、评审者和人类内容评分者等多种角色，但每种范式本质上都是一个测量问题（通过量表工具由评分者探测对象的潜在属性）。标准评估实践往往忽视各核心组件（对象、工具、评分者）对最终结果的贡献，导致研究者对"实际测量的内容"理解受限。

Method: 将RMT应用于LLM-as-rater范式的案例研究。使用Measuring Hate Speech语料库（该语料库的构念本身就是在RMT框架下构建的），对九个涵盖不同模型家族和能力水平的LLM产生的标注数据，拟合一系列多面Rasch模型（many-facet Rasch models），从多个维度系统比较LLM评分者与人类评分者的行为差异。

Result: 分析表明，LLM与人类评分者在五个维度上存在系统性差异：评分严格度（severity）、题目层面的校准（item-level calibration）、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性（target-identity sensitivity）以及评分量表的使用方式。这些差异在标准评估实践中会被掩盖，只有在RMT分解框架下才能显现。

Conclusion: RMT应当被纳入LLM评估的标准工具库中，无论是在LLM-as-examinee、LLM-as-judge还是LLM-as-rater范式中，它都能提供标准实践无法揭示的细粒度诊断信息，帮助识别失准的测量和评分者偏差。

Abstract: LLMs now sit on every side of evaluation: as examinees scored on benchmarks, judges of other models' outputs, and raters of human-generated content. Each paradigm can be viewed as a measurement problem, where a latent property of an object is probed with items from an instrument (e.g., benchmark) by raters. Standard evaluation practices often neglect the contributions of each core component to the end result, limiting our understanding of what is being measured. Rasch measurement theory (RMT) is well-suited to this kind of problem. RMT decomposes ordinal ratings into separable facets on a common scale. It further provides a battery of diagnostics that can identify miscalibrated measurements and rater biases. We present a case study of RMT applied to the LLM-as-rater paradigm using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, whose construct was itself built under RMT. We fit a series of many-facet Rasch models to annotations from nine LLMs spanning families and capability levels. Our analyses show that LLMs systematically differ from human raters in severity, item-level calibration, question-order robustness, target-identity sensitivity, and rating scale use, which all would be obscured by standard evaluation practice. Overall, we argue that RMT belongs in the toolkit for evaluating LLM-as-examinee, -judge, and -rater paradigms.

</details>


### [229] [Not All Explanations Are Sought: Information-Seeking Psychology for Human-Centered XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27464)
*Andrea Beretta,Salvatore Rinzivillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人本可解释AI（HCXAI）应借鉴信息寻求心理学，基于Sharot和Sunstein的框架，认为用户根据工具性、享乐性和认知性三种预期效用决定是否参与解释，并通过认知偏差的透镜评估这些效用，导致过度或不足的信息寻求两种失败模式，尤以代理式AI系统为甚。论文倡导从"提供解释"转向"设计被寻求的解释"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCXAI研究主要关注如何做出可用的解释，但忽略了用户是否真正愿意去寻求和参与解释这一根本问题。论文指出理解用户何时及为何寻求解释是提升人机信任与决策质量的关键。

Method: 这是一个立场论文（position paper），无实证实验。作者整合Sharot和Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架（工具性、享乐性、认知性效用），引入六种认知偏差（控制幻觉、自动化偏见、不切实际乐观、影响偏差、过度自信、确认偏差）作为效用评估的透镜，并分析两种失败模式及其在代理式AI系统中的特殊挑战。

Result: 论文提出一个理论框架：用户对解释的参与决策由三种预期效用的评估驱动，而认知偏差会扭曲这些评估，导致过度信息寻求（注意力碎片化但决策未改善）和不足信息寻求（关键风险与误解未被审视）两种失败模式。在代理式AI中，解释需支持级联行动预期、风险评估和干预时机的判断。

Conclusion: 应将信息寻求心理学整合进HCXAI设计，从"使解释可用"转向"使解释被寻求"——即设计能够主动预测并回应"何时"和"为何"用户想要了解的系统，而非仅提供静态可用的解释。

Abstract: This position paper argues that human-centered explainable AI (HCXAI) should incorporate insights from the psychology of information seeking. Drawing on Sharot and Sunstein's framework of information-seeking motives, we propose that people evaluate whether to engage with explanations based on three types of expected utility: instrumental (will it help me act better?), hedonic (will it make me feel better?), and cognitive (will it improve my understanding?). Each utility is estimated through a lens shaped by well-documented cognitive biases, including illusion of control, automation bias, unrealistic optimism, impact bias, overconfidence, and confirmation bias. These biases can lead to two failure modes: excessive information-seeking that fragments attention without improving decisions, and insufficient information-seeking that leaves critical risks and misunderstandings unexamined. This challenge is particularly acute for agentic AI systems, where explanations must support not just understanding a single output but anticipating cascading actions, assessing risks, and deciding when to intervene. By integrating information-seeking psychology into HCXAI, we advocate for a shift from making explanations available to making them sought: designing systems that account for when and why users actually want to know.

</details>


### [230] [Retrieving Relations, Detecting Fallacies: A RAG Approach to Political Debate Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27471)
*Deborah Dore,Greta Damo,Elena Cabrio,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于论证关系引导的检索增强方法（guided retrieval-augmented methodology），利用论证结构中支持与攻击关系动态引导相关文档的检索，用于谬误检测与分类。在ElecDeb60to20基准上，通过42种检索配置和14个模型的实验，在15GB政治文档知识库上检索，谬误检测macro-F1达0.864，分类达0.725，显著优于非检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谬误检测在政治辩论等敏感高风险的语境中至关重要，但检测谬误需要超越表面文本的上下文知识（包括世界知识和论证关系）。已有研究表明论证话语结构能提升分类性能，但传统方法仅将结构编码为静态分类器特征，缺乏灵活性。作者旨在解决这一局限，利用论证结构动态引导检索过程。

Method: 提出一种引导式检索增强方法（guided retrieval-augmented methodology），利用论证关系中支持（support）和攻击（attack）关系动态引导相关文档的检索。具体包括：1）利用论证结构信息来引导检索方向；2）在15GB政治相关文档知识库上执行检索；3）在ElecDeb60to20基准上系统评估42种检索配置和14种模型。

Result: 在ElecDeb60to20基准上，谬误检测macro-F1提升至0.864，谬误分类macro-F1提升至0.725，均优于非检索基线。结果表明，当检索过程受论证关系引导时，外部知识的引入能显著增强谬误检测与分类性能。

Conclusion: 论证引导的检索增强方法能够有效提升谬误检测与分类的性能，说明将论证话语结构动态融入检索过程比静态特征编码更具优势，外部知识在谬误分析中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Fallacies are arguments that employ invalid reasoning, making their automatic detection critical in sensitive contexts such as high-stakes political debates, where public opinion is shaped. Spotting a fallacious argument requires contextual knowledge beyond its pure surface text. This entails world knowledge pertaining to the subject matter under discussion, as well as knowledge of the relationships that exist between arguments within the argumentative discourse. Prior work on fallacy analysis has shown that argumentative discourse structure can beneficially improve classification performance. However, such structure is typically encoded only as static classifier features, limiting its flexibility. Building on this intuition while addressing this limitation, we introduce a guided retrieval-augmented methodology for fallacy detection and classification that leverages argumentative relations of support and attack to dynamically steer the extraction of relevant documents. We evaluate our approach on the ElecDeb60to20 benchmark across 42 retrieval configurations and 14 models, performing retrieval over a 15GB knowledge base of collected political-related documents. Our approach improves macro-F1 up to 0.864 for fallacy detection and up to 0.725 for classification over non-retrieval baselines. These results show that incorporating external knowledge significantly enhances fallacy detection and classification when retrieval is argumentatively guided.

</details>


### [231] [LLM-Augmented Causal Discovery: Probabilistic Fusion of Edge Existence and Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27472)
*Neville K. Kitson,Anthony Constantinou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率依赖图（PDG）来融合贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）与大型语言模型（LLM）的因果知识，通过将每条边表示为有向、无向和不存在的概率分布进行加权平均融合。在26个基准网络上，简单50/50融合在22个网络中优于任一单独来源，F1平均提升0.056（p<0.001），且两种来源呈现互补性：BNSL提供高召回率的边骨架，LLM提供高精度的边的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNSL从观测数据中学习贝叶斯网络结构时，在边的方向识别（orientation identifiability）上存在固有限制；而LLM虽具有广泛的因果知识，但可靠性不足。两种来源各有优缺点，需要一种有效的表示和融合机制来结合它们以提升因果图学习的准确性。

Method: 提出概率依赖图（PDG）表示法，将每条边关联一个覆盖有向、无向和不存在的状态分布，通过加权平均实现两种来源的融合。实证上组合三种BNSL算法（FGES、Tabu、PC）与三种LLM（Gemini、Claude、GPT），使用多个提示词和随机种子，在26个基准网络上评估。

Result: 简单50/50融合在26个网络中有22个的F1指标优于任一单独来源，平均改善0.056（p<0.001，统计显著）。分析表明两种来源互补：BNSL贡献高召回率的边骨架（80%对LLM的60%），LLM贡献高精度的方向判断（96%对BNSL的77%）。

Conclusion: 将两种来源表示为关于边存在性和方向的概率不确定性，是一种实用且有效的提升因果图准确性的方法。融合策略虽然简单，但能充分利用两种知识的互补优势。

Abstract: Bayesian network structure learning (BNSL) from observational data struggles with orientation identifiability, while large language models (LLMs) offer broad but often unreliable causal knowledge. We propose combining these complementary sources through a novel representation, termed Probabilistic Dependency Graphs (PDGs). In a PDG, each edge is associated with a distribution over directed, undirected, and absent states, enabling fusion via weighted averaging. We evaluate this approach on 26 benchmark networks, combining ensembles of three BNSL algorithms (FGES, Tabu, PC) with three LLMs (Gemini, Claude, GPT) across multiple prompts and random seeds. A simple 50/50 fusion improves F1 over the better of either source alone in 22 of 26 networks, with a statistically significant mean improvement of $0.056$ $(p<0.001)$. Analysis reveals that the two sources play complementary roles: BNSL contributes a high-recall edge skeleton (80\% vs 60\% for LLM), while LLM contributes accurate edge orientation (96\% vs 77\% for BNSL). Our results show that representing both sources as probabilistic uncertainty over edge existence and orientation is a practical and effective way to improve causal graph accuracy.

</details>


### [232] [Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine: A Scientific Agent for PDE Discovery with Unknown Spatial Coefficient Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27475)
*YuJie Huang,WenWu He,ZhuoEr Lin,Congcong Liu,Dong Liang,Zhuo-Xu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HER-PDE是一个科学智能体框架，用于在异构介质中发现偏微分方程（PDE），能够同时识别控制算子及其未知的空间系数场。该框架通过在两条不同激励下的含噪轨迹上提出、评估和精炼表达式树假设，在五个二维系统中均成功恢复生成算子，并在九个未知系数场上达到中位Pearson相关系数约0.85、中位相对L2误差约0.28。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构介质中发现PDE面临双重耦合挑战：改变场分布会改变微分定律，而过于灵活的场可能掩盖单一轨迹上的结构误差。现有方法无法同时解决算子识别和空间场参数化这两个相互关联的任务，因此需要一种无需预设系数场参数形式的方法来恢复异构控制定律。

Method: HER-PDE采用假设-评估-精炼（Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine）循环的科学智能体框架。Agent分析由不同激励产生的两条含噪轨迹，提出完整的表达式树假设，结合创造性结构探索与局部候选精炼。其假设评估接口（HEI）仅估计每个假设中明确声明的场，不添加缺失项，并通过双向交叉激励迁移对结构进行评分。最终选定的定律在封存的未来时间区间上接受审计。

Result: 在五个受控二维系统（含5%相对高斯状态噪声）中，Agent在所有五种情况下均恢复生成算子，包括等价的符号场和乘积律参数化。在九个未知系数场上，恢复的场达到中位Pearson相关系数约0.85、中位相对L2误差约0.28。

Conclusion: 结果表明，智能体引导的假设精炼能够在不为空间系数预设参数形式的情况下恢复异构控制定律，为解决耦合的算子发现和场估计问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Discovering PDEs in heterogeneous media requires jointly identifying the governing operator and the unknown spatial fields that parameterize it. These tasks are coupled: changing field placement changes the differential law, while a sufficiently flexible field can conceal structural error on a single trajectory. We present Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine for PDE Discovery (HER-PDE), a scientific-agent framework that discovers compositional PDE structure together with nonparametric, time-invariant coefficient fields. The Agent analyzes two noisy trajectories generated by different excitations, proposes complete expression-tree hypotheses, and combines creative structural exploration with local candidate refinement. Its Hypothesis Evaluation Interface (HEI) estimates only the fields explicitly declared in each hypothesis, never adds missing terms, and scores structures by bidirectional cross-excitation transfer. The selected law is subsequently audited on a sealed temporal interval. Across five controlled two-dimensional systems observed with 5 percent relative Gaussian state noise, the Agent recovers the generating operator in all five cases, including equivalent signed-field and product-rule parameterizations. Across nine unknown coefficient fields, the recovered fields attain a median Pearson correlation of approximately 0.85 and a median relative L2 error of approximately 0.28. These results show that agent-guided hypothesis refinement can recover heterogeneous governing laws without prescribing a parametric form for their spatial coefficients.

</details>


### [233] [Class-Based Heuristic Selection for Solving the Flying Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27476)
*Sanyar Ahmadi,Pedram Asadzadeh,Amanj Khorramian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CBHA*（基于类别的启发式A*）算法，通过在双列飞行积木谜题这一NP完全空间规划微世界中，整合移动约束、七个互斥状态类别及对应的可采纳启发式，以及类条件平局打破机制，将搜索成功率从标准A*的39%提升至93.4%，节点扩展减少87.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用启发式算法无法利用受限空间域中的结构约束，导致在困难实例上搜索性能急剧退化。这一问题广泛存在于仓库物流、自主导航、多智能体路径规划及块状重定位系统等物理约束空间规划任务中。

Method: CBHA*算法包含三个核心组件：(1) 通用移动约束，捕捉空闲单元稀缺时的最小位移成本；(2) 形式化运动学分类法，将状态空间划分为七个互斥类别，基于空缺率和目标块几何形状推导可采纳启发式；(3) 类条件平局打破机制，在深度优先与垂直距离排序之间动态切换以克服f值平台区。

Result: 在146个基准实例上，CBHA*成功率达到93.4%（对比深度优先A*的64%、标准A*的39%、BFS的17%），相对于标准A*节点扩展减少87.98%，平均有效分支因子约为3。

Conclusion: 类别触发的自适应启发式是高效空间规划的普适性机制，能够结构化地泛化到物理约束系统，为受瓶颈几何约束的空间规划问题提供了一种原理性解决方案。

Abstract: Heuristic search underlies planning in autonomous systems ranging from warehouse logistics to robotic navigation, yet generic heuristics fail to exploit the structural constraints that govern constrained spatial domains, causing search performance to degrade catastrophically on harder instances. We study this problem through the two-column Flying Block Puzzle, a rigorously NP-complete spatial planning microworld whose bottleneck geometry mirrors clearance-to-size constraints encountered in multi-agent path finding, autonomous vehicle navigation, and block relocation systems. We introduce the Class-Based Heuristic A* (CBHA*) algorithm, which integrates a General Move Constraint to capture minimum displacement costs when vacant units are scarce, a formal kinematic taxonomy partitioning the state space into seven mutually exclusive classes with provably admissible heuristics based on vacancy ratio and goal-piece geometry, and a class-conditional tie-breaking mechanism that dynamically switches between depth-priority and vertical-distance ordering to overcome f-value plateaus. Over 146 benchmark instances, CBHA* achieves a 93.4% success rate against 64% for Depth-Prioritized A*, 39% for Standard A*, and 17% for BFS, while reducing node expansions by 87.98% relative to Standard A* and sustaining an average effective branching factor of approximately 3, demonstrating that class-triggered adaptive heuristics constitute a principled mechanism for efficient spatial planning that generalizes structurally to physical constraint systems.

</details>


### [234] [Benchmarking General Mobile Assistants in Challenging Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27477)
*Yiqi Zhu,Feiyu Gao,Jiaxing Fan,Jiahui Zeng,Minggang Wu,Chenliang Li,Haiyang Xu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GMA是一个用于评估通用移动助手在真实世界场景中表现的新型基准测试，引入了7个基于开源项目的应用和300个跨四个难度级别的任务，评估了8个前沿模型并发现任务复杂度增加时性能显著下降，同时通过受控消融实验研究了代理框架设计对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准如AndroidWorld和MobileWorld为移动智能体评估提供了基础，但其应用覆盖范围和任务设计未能充分捕捉现实移动使用的多样性和复杂性，需要一个更具挑战性和全面性的基准来评估移动助手。

Method: GMA包含7个基于开源项目的应用（覆盖生活方式分享和旅行规划等领域）、300个任务分布在四个难度级别（从原子操作到复杂多步骤工作流）；评估8个前沿模型；在共享环境、模型和任务分类下进行受控消融实验，研究代理框架选择（包括上下文保留和显式状态跟踪）。

Result: 随着任务复杂度的增加，模型性能显著下降，当前智能体远未达到可靠处理真实用户需求的程度；适当的框架设计可以显著提升性能，尤其在困难工作流上，但具体设计的有效性因基础模型而异。

Conclusion: GMA通过扩展应用覆盖范围和任务复杂度补充了现有基准，为评估移动智能体以及研究框架设计如何支持复杂移动工作流中的可靠执行提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Graphical user interfaces have emerged as an important environment for evaluating autonomous AI agents on multimodal interactive tasks. Existing benchmarks such as AndroidWorld and MobileWorld provide strong foundations for mobile agent evaluation, but their application coverage and task design do not yet fully capture the diversity and complexity of realistic mobile use. We present GMA, a benchmark for evaluating general mobile assistants in challenging real-world scenarios. GMA introduces seven applications based on open-source projects, spanning domains such as lifestyle sharing and travel planning, and 300 tasks across four difficulty tiers, from atomic actions to complex multi-step workflows. We evaluate eight frontier models and find that performance declines substantially as task complexity increases, with current agents remaining far from reliably handling realistic user requirements. We further conduct controlled ablation studies of agent harness choices, including context retention and explicit state tracking, under a shared environment, model setting, and task taxonomy. Results show that appropriate harness design can meaningfully improve performance, particularly on demanding workflows, while the effectiveness of specific designs can vary across foundation models. Overall, GMA complements existing benchmarks by expanding application coverage and task complexity, providing a challenging testbed for evaluating mobile agents and studying how harness design supports reliable execution in complex mobile workflows.

</details>


### [235] [Effectiveness of IoT and Deep Learning for Detection and Severity Assessment of Postelectrotermes militaris in Tea Plantations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27480)
*D. K. C. Senevirathna,A. A. E. Nanayakkara,H. M. C. K. Kulathunga,J. K. D. P. Nadula,R. M. Mapatuna,Malithi Nawarathne,Jaliya L. Wijayaraja,P. D. Senanayake,Samitha Vidhanaarachchi,Kalpani Manathunga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于物联网与深度学习的声学监测框架，用于茶园上地活木白蚁（ULWT）的早期检测和严重程度评估。通过Raspberry Pi IoT设备的麦克风采集茶树干部音频信号，利用傅里叶变换谱图训练CNN进行侵染分类，并结合声学幅度、附近侵染植株数量和地理空间映射构建加权严重程度模型。现场试验在Pundaluoya茶园验证了可行性，CNN在测试集上达到81.5%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白蚁（Upcountry Live Wood Termite）侵染在茶园中若未被及时发现会造成重大损害，传统的检测方法可能难以实现早期和大范围监测。因此需要一种非侵入性、可扩展的物联网监测方案来及早发现并评估白蚁侵染的严重程度，以便管理者及时采取针对性防治措施。

Method: 研究采用高灵敏度麦克风连接Raspberry Pi构成的IoT设备，非侵入式采集茶树干声学信号并记录地理坐标。信号经裁剪、重采样和分段处理后获得2,000个10秒样本（1,000健康+1,000侵染），按1,600/200/200比例分为训练、验证和测试集。使用傅里叶变换生成的谱图训练CNN进行分类和侵染概率估计。同时构建加权严重程度模型，综合CNN概率、平均声学幅度及5米范围内附近侵染植株数量，并通过地理空间映射可视化侵染分布。

Result: 在受ULWT影响的Pundaluoya茶园现场试验中，框架在现实环境噪声下展现出可行性。测试集上CNN达到81.5%准确率、80.6%精确率、83.0%召回率、81.8% F1分数和0.819 ROC-AUC。除二元侵染检测外，框架还实现了基于侵染概率、声学幅度和邻近侵染植株的定量严重程度评估，并可生成严重程度和地理空间输出。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅实现了白蚁侵染的二元检测，还引入了定量严重程度评估方法，其产出的严重程度和地理空间信息可辅助茶园管理者识别高风险区域、优先安排现场检查，并实施更及时、更精准的防控措施，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Tea plantations are vulnerable to Postelectrotermes militaris, commonly known as the Upcountry Live Wood Termite (ULWT), which can cause substantial damage when infestations remain undetected. This study proposes an IoT-enabled acoustic monitoring framework integrated with deep learning for early detection and severity assessment of ULWT infestations in tea plantations.
  Research Method: Audio signals were captured non-invasively from tea trunks using a high-sensitivity microphone connected to a Raspberry Pi-based IoT device, with geographic coordinates recorded for spatial tracking. After trimming, resampling, and segmentation, 2,000 ten-second samples were obtained, comprising 1,000 healthy and 1,000 infested samples, and divided into 1,600 training, 200 validation, and 200 test samples. The dataset used in this study is publicly available on Kaggle (Senevirathna et al. 2026). Fourier-derived spectrograms trained a CNN for infestation classification and probability estimation. A weighted severity model combined CNN probability, mean acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants within 5 m, with geospatial mapping used to visualize infestation distribution.
  Findings and Values: Field trials in a ULWT-affected tea plantation in Pundaluoya demonstrated feasibility under realistic environmental noise. On the held-out test set, the CNN achieved 81.5% accuracy, 80.6% precision, 83.0% recall, 81.8% F1-score, and 0.819 ROC-AUC. Beyond binary infestation detection, the framework introduced quantitative severity assessment using infestation probability, acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants. The resulting severity and geospatial outputs can support plantation managers in identifying high-risk areas, prioritizing field inspections, and implementing more timely and targeted control measures.

</details>


### [236] [Context Localization for Generalized Level-Based Evaluation in Knowledge-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27482)
*Ondrej Hutník,Natália Puškárová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识系统中基于广义级别评估的上下文局部化问题，提出了一个框架，其中定义在事实、规则、案例、准则或证据单元上的结构化非负分数通过可接受知识上下文上的条件聚合测试进行评估。主要定理刻画了过滤得分与通过交集局部化上下文等价的充要条件，即单调性和还原性两个结构条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识型系统中需要评估结构化非负分数（如事实、规则、证据单元上的分数），并通过条件聚合测试在可接受的知识上下文上进行评估。一个关键问题是：当用上下文B过滤得分时，是否等价于将可接受上下文与B求交集进行局部化？这种一致性对于上下文相关的证据选择、非加性支持评估和基于级别的知识聚合至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的理论刻画。

Method: 本文构建了广义级别评估的框架模型，其中广义级别度量在达到规定级别的所有上下文上最大化一个单调集函数。作者证明了该一致性对所有单调集函数成立当且仅当满足两个结构条件：（1）关于上下文的单调性；（2）排除B外部正局部化支持的还原性质。此外，作者分析了产生还原性的逐点和块生成机制，并将结果推广到参数化系统。

Result: 主要定理表明，上下文过滤与上下文局部化的一致性对所有单调集函数成立，当且仅当满足上下文单调性和还原性两个结构条件。文中还分析了逐点和块生成机制如何产生还原性质，并将结果扩展至参数化系统，解释为上下文依赖证据选择、非加性支持评估和基于级别的知识聚合的稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 该研究为知识系统中的上下文局部化与过滤等价性提供了完整的理论刻画，确立了两个必要的结构条件，并展示了其在非加性支持评估和级别化知识聚合中的实际意义，为上下文依赖的知识评估系统提供了稳定性判据。

Abstract: We study context localization for generalized level-based evaluation in knowledge-based systems. The framework models situations where a structured nonnegative score, defined on facts, rules, cases, criteria or evidence units, is evaluated through conditional aggregation tests on admissible knowledge contexts. The generalized level measure maximizes a monotone set function over all contexts whose aggregated support reaches a prescribed level.
